  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统无人机主要用于感知任务；空中操作研究通常忽略下洗气流或将其视为干扰。本文探索能否将下洗气流转化为操作工具。

Method: 设计无人机足球运球任务，建立简化的下洗气流动力学模型，并基于该模型训练强化学习策略。

Result: 训练得到的策略能够控制无人机运球，且可迁移到真实世界部署。

Conclusion: 研究表明，下洗气流可用于拓展多旋翼无人机的操作能力，为空中移动操作提供了新思路。

Abstract: Although multicopter drones are traditionally designed for "perception-only" tasks, like mapping and exploration, recent work has sought to develop Unmanned Aerial Manipulators (UAMs) to solve mobile manipulation tasks. Aerial manipulation performance can be impacted by "downwash," the airflow produced by propellers, but current state-of-the-art UAMs either ignore downwash or treat it as a disturbance. Instead, is it possible to actively use downwash as a tool during manipulation? We design a drone soccer task to explore the feasibility of downwash-based manipulation. Specifically, we develop a simplified downwash dynamics model which we use to train an RL policy to dribble a soccer ball. We further demonstrate that our policy transfers to real world deployment. This work provides key insights into novel manipulation capabilities for multicopters.

</details>


### [74] [Data-Efficient Adaptation of a Driving VLA to Class 8 Trucks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38570)
*Satyajeet Das,Aaron Buxbaum,Niels Joubert,Gaurav S. Sukhatme*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出“先适配、再引导”方案，将现成视觉—语言—动作模型迁移至 Class 8 重型卡车的复杂道路场景，并提升轨迹预测精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 面向乘用车训练的模型难以直接迁移到重型卡车，尤其是在施工区域等非结构化场景；从零训练卡车模型成本较高，因此需要数据高效的迁移方法。

Method: 适配阶段以 NVIDIA Alpamayo 1.5 为基础，仅微调其动作生成模块，使用数百个真实场景样本。引导阶段冻结适配后的模型，加入 Flow Velocity Steering（FVS）残差模块，在每个生成步骤修正动作空间中的流速度。

Result: 在场景不重叠的留出集上，针对性微调使 6.4 秒预测全时域的单候选 ADE 和 FDE 均降低一半以上。加入 FVS 后，ADE 和 FDE 分别进一步降低 13.9% 和 16.5%。相同数据预算下，针对性监督的 ADE 比通用卡车驾驶监督低 19%–26%；其表现也可与使用约 65 倍通用驾驶场景微调的模型相竞争。

Conclusion: 实验支持“先适配、再引导”作为面向 Class 8 卡车的高数据效率领域迁移方案，能够改善复杂场景中的轨迹预测表现。

Abstract: Class 8 trucks differ from passenger cars in geometry, dynamics, and maneuvering requirements. As a result, vision-language-action (VLA) models trained for passenger vehicles do not readily transfer to Class 8 trucks, particularly in unstructured scenarios such as accident scenes and construction zones. Rather than training a truck-driving VLA from scratch, we propose an adapt-then-steer strategy that adapts an off-the-shelf VLA to generate trajectories for Class-8 trucks in these challenging scenarios. In the adapt stage, we use NVIDIA's Alpamayo 1.5 as the base model, fine-tuning only its action-generation stack on a few hundred real-world construction and accident-related highway scenarios. In the steer stage, we introduce Flow Velocity Steering (FVS) to further refine the model's predictions while holding the adapted VLA fixed. FVS is a compact, flow-time-conditioned residual module that adds learned corrections to the action-space flow velocity used to update the action sequence at each generation step. In open-loop evaluation on a scenario-disjoint held-out set, targeted fine-tuning more than halves single-candidate average displacement error (ADE) and final displacement error (FDE) over the entire 6.4 s horizon compared to the base model. Using the same targeted demonstrations, FVS further reduces the fine-tuned model's full-horizon ADE and FDE by 13.9% and 16.5%, respectively. At matched data budgets, targeted supervision yields 19-26% lower full-horizon ADE than general truck-driving supervision, while the targeted model remains competitive with a model fine-tuned on approximately 65 times as many general truck-driving scenarios. These results support adapt-then-steer for data-efficient vehicle-domain transfer to Class 8 trucks. Our project website is available at this https URL .

</details>


### [75] [Systematically Exploring the Capabilities of GPT-6 Astra as Embodied Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38537)
*Galbot Team,Xuchuan Chen,Xiaoqian Cheng,Yu Deng,Lihe Ding,Shaocong Dong,Xiangjun Gao,Haozhe Jia,Zekai Li,Zhoujian Li,Yunrui Lian,Sikai Liang,Chenghuai Lin,Dairu Liu,Jiahang Liu,Qingtao Liu,Yuxuan Ma,Zekun Qi,Jiayi Su,He Wang,Ruochen Xu,Tianyu Xu,Xudong Xu,Zhe Xu,Mi Yan,Siming Yan,Li Yi,Ruixi Yu,Jinlu Zhang,Yintianrun Zhang,Zhikai Zhang,Zhizheng Zhang,Yixin Zheng,Weiyi Zhu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 论文评估 GPT-6 Astra 作为通用具身策略的能力，发现其在任务决策、导航及与预训练控制器协作方面表现有用，但精细物理控制的可靠性与推理延迟仍是主要瓶颈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 评估 Astra 是否能超越高层规划，直接生成机器人动作，并在多种具身任务中充当通用策略；同时考察其与学习型策略协作及基于反馈的适应能力。

Method: 在抓取操作、灵巧操作、移动操作、导航、运动控制和人形机器人运动操作六个领域开展评估，比较直接控制、混合控制及策略协作等设置，并记录任务成功率、模型调用次数和推理耗时。

Result: 混合控制在 RoboDojo 子集上的成功率为 48%，在 DexJoCo 十次经验引导试验中为 50%，在 RoboCasa365 上为 38.7%。导航方面，指令跟随成功率为 92%，物体搜索为 82%，但搜索路线存在明显绕行。人形机器人运动操作中，结合预训练全身控制器后，Astra 在 30 项任务中的 13 项优于基线。运动控制方面，五次连续障碍课程尝试均未到达终点。RoboDojo 每种条件评估 50 个实例时，策略辅助控制和直接控制分别消耗 6.248 亿和 11.32 亿 token；一次 30 秒运动控制运行需调用模型 250 次，平均每次耗时 39.86 秒，且推理期间物理仿真暂停。

Conclusion: Astra 已能提供有价值的任务级决策，并在部分领域通过与专用控制器协作取得较好表现，但尚不能稳定完成密集、精细的物理控制。较高的推理延迟和 token 消耗进一步限制其实时部署，凸显了高级决策能力与可靠低层控制之间的差距。

Abstract: GPT-6 Astra exhibits a remarkable ability to generate numerical robot actions, extending its role beyond high-level planning. To assess Astra's capabilities as general-purpose embodied policies, we conduct comprehensive evaluations across six domains, examining direct control, cooperation with learned policies, and feedback-driven adaptation. In gripper manipulation, Astra can correct task targets and prepare contact conditions for subsequent policy execution; hybrid control with {\pi}0.5 achieves 48% success on the evaluated RoboDojo subset. In dexterous manipulation, hybrid control achieves 50% success in ten experience-guided DexJoCo trials, while direct in-hand control struggles to coordinate finger contacts. In mobile manipulation, hybrid control reaches 38.7% success on the evaluated RoboCasa365. In navigation, Astra leads our local comparisons, reaching 92% success on RxR instruction following and 82% on HM3D object search, although search incurs substantial detours. In locomotion, dense motion-reference generation remains unreliable: none of five sequential attempts on a single obstacle course reaches the goal, despite improvements in stability and forward progress. In humanoid loco-manipulation, Astra exceeds baseline methods on 13 of 30 HumanoidBench tasks with pretrained whole-body controllers. These findings reveal a gap between useful task decisions and reliable physical control. Inference latency further constrains practical control: across 50 RoboDojo instances per condition, policy-assisted and direct control consume 624.8 million and 1.132 billion tokens. A 30-second locomotion run requires 250 model calls averaging 39.86 seconds each, with physics paused during inference.

</details>


### [76] [Behavioral Persistence and Incomplete Functional Transfer of Co-evolved Communication in Evolutionary Robotics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38527)
*Fernando Montes-Gonzalez*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 研究评估了无需重新训练、将二维仿真中协同进化的通信协议直接迁移到三维实体机器人环境的可行性。迁移仅部分成功且具有明显不对称性，表明通信信号保持并不等同于任务行为成功。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究仿真中涌现的社会通信协议能否在真实物理约束下直接复现，并分析迁移失败究竟源于信号转换问题，还是源于导航和生态条件发生变化。

Method: 使用两台类e-puck机器人执行带社会信号的觅食任务。机器人由带残差连接的GRU网络控制，直接采用仿真训练权重，不进行重新训练。研究对传感器与执行器转换层进行三项稳定性修正，其中包括依据训练残差权重可测不对称性校准饥饿项；随后在部分实验中向社会通信通道加入显式方向信息，并比较机器人轨迹与任务完成情况。

Result: 经过修正后，迁移仍表现为部分且不对称：在30个测试随机种子中，一台机器人仅有1次到达食物源，另一台一次也未到达；按照两台机器人均进入食物区域定义的任务成功未实现。加入显式方向信息后，接收机器人轨迹发生可观察变化，并在若干特定情形下有所改善，但仍不足以使第二台机器人到达食物源。

Conclusion: 直接迁移涌现通信协议的主要瓶颈不只在信号表示或转换，还在于物理环境中的导航能力及任务生态条件与仿真环境不一致。要实现可靠迁移，可能需要同时保持通信机制以及协议演化所依赖的感知、运动和导航条件。

Abstract: This work evaluates the direct transfer of a co-evolved communication protocol from a 2D simulation to a 3D physical environment, without retraining the network weights. Two e-puck-type robots, controlled by a GRU network with residual connection, were evaluated in a food-seeking task with social signaling. The sensory and motor translation layer required three corrections for stable physical operation, including the calibration of a hunger term based on a measurable asymmetry in the trained residual weights. Even with these corrections, the transfer was partial and asymmetric: one agent reached the food source in one of thirty tested seeds, while the other did not reach it in any. Task success was measured by both agents reaching the food area. An additional experiment incorporating explicit directional information in the social channel produced observable changes in the trajectory of the receiving agent and improvements in several specific cases. However, these improvements were not enough to allow the second agent to reach the food source, suggesting that the limitation may not be explained solely by signal translation, but also by the ability to navigate under the new physical constraints. The results suggest that successful transfer of emergent communication may depend not only on preserving the signaling process itself, but also on preserving the ecological and navigational conditions under which the protocol evolved.

</details>


### [77] [Onboard Vision and MPC Navigation for Underwater Robots: An Open BlueROV2 Platform for Multi-Robot Experiments & Docking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38511)
*Victor Nan Fernandez-Ayala,Wiktor Kowalczyk,Cezary Banaszek,Dimos V. Dimarogonas*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 论文提出一套开源 BlueROV2 水下自主导航与对接平台，结合视觉感知、状态估计和非线性模型预测控制，并提供仿真与实体测试资源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 水下机器人在狭窄环境中运行，需要可靠的感知、状态估计和控制能力；可复现的开发与测试工具也有助于推进相关研究。

Method: 平台搭载 NVIDIA Jetson Orin NX 和 Intel RealSense D435i 立体相机，通过水下标定的立体深度与实时目标检测测量邻近 BlueROV2 的相对位置；使用基于四元数的估计器融合外部位姿和惯性测量，并以六自由度非线性模型构建 NMPC 控制器，跟踪导航与对接轨迹。另提供 PX4 SITL、Gazebo 多机器人仿真工具及低成本实体对接站。

Result: 实验评估了水下感知、机载计算性能、状态估计、轨迹跟踪和自主对接。摘要未提供具体量化结果。

Conclusion: 该工作提供了一套集成视觉、估计与 NMPC 的开源水下导航和对接平台，以及配套仿真与实体验证资源，支持可复现的开发和评估。

Abstract: Autonomous underwater robots require robust perception, estimation and control to operate in confined environments. This paper presents an open-source BlueROV2 platform combining onboard vision with nonlinear Model Predictive Control (NMPC) for autonomous navigation and docking. The platform integrates an NVIDIA Jetson Orin NX and an Intel RealSense D435i stereo camera in a modular pressure housing. Underwater-calibrated stereo depth and realtime object detection provide relative position measurements of nearby BlueROV2 vehicles in the camera and body frames. A quaternion-based estimator fuses external pose and inertial measurements, while an NMPC controller based on a nonlinear six-degree-of-freedom model tracks planned navigation and docking trajectories. To support reproducible development, we also provide open-source physics-based PX4 SITL and Gazebo environments, multi-robot simulation tools and a lowcost physical docking station. Experiments evaluate underwater perception, onboard computational performance, state estimation, trajectory tracking and autonomous docking.

</details>


### [78] [CADeT: Causal-Aware Deformation Transmission for Indirect Robotic Manipulation of Soft Tissue](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38483)
*Junlei Hu,Dominic Jones,Pietro Valdastri*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 CADeT 框架，通过主动感知识别组织间的变形传递模式，并在线估计黏附雅可比矩阵，以实现深部解剖结构的间接形状控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 微创手术中，机器人无法直接触及的深部组织，其变形会受到中间组织的空间滤波；仅凭被动观测，难以区分产生相似运动响应的解耦模式与阻断模式。

Method: 将结构因果模型与主动感知结合，推断潜在传递模式并在线估计状态相关的黏附雅可比矩阵。常规操作用于更新模式置信度；若歧义持续，则选择探测动作以提高模式可区分性。随后将模式置信度和学习到的雅可比矩阵纳入置信度感知的模型预测控制器。

Result: 在仿真及 da Vinci 研究平台（dVRK）上，使用仿体和离体猪组织进行验证。相较于所评估的无模型与基于模型基线，CADeT 的模式识别准确率更高，形状误差收敛更快。

Conclusion: 实验结果表明，在本文评估条件下，主动感知有助于改善变形传递模式识别与间接变形控制。

Abstract: Indirect manipulation of deep-seated deformable anatomy inaccessible to the robot is challenging in robot-assisted minimally invasive surgery (RAMIS) because intervening tissues spatially filter deformation transmission. Passive observations can be ambiguous because the Decoupled and Blocked modes may produce similar motion responses. We propose CADeT, a causal-aware deformation transmission framework that integrates structural causal model (SCM) with active sensing to infer a latent transmission mode and estimate a state-dependent adhesion Jacobian online. During normal manipulation, control actions update the mode belief; when ambiguity persists, an additional probing action is selected to improve mode distinguishability. The mode belief and learned Jacobian are incorporated into a belief-aware model predictive controller for indirect target-shape control. Validation in simulation and on the da Vinci research kit (dVRK), using phantom and ex vivo porcine tissues, shows higher mode-identification accuracy and faster shape-error convergence than the evaluated model-free and model-based baselines. These results show that active sensing improves mode identification and indirect deformation control under the evaluated conditions.

</details>


### [79] [L1-MPPI: L1 Adaptive Model Predictive Path Integral for Agile UAV Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38467)
*Lukáš Kotek,Ondřej Procházka,Vojtěch Vonásek,Martin Saska,Robert Pěnička*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出 L1-MPPI，将 L1 自适应控制与模型预测路径积分控制结合，提升高速无人机在模型不确定和外部扰动下的轨迹跟踪能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 MPPI 无人机控制方法通常未充分建模空气动力学、载荷变化及低层电机控制器动态，因而在质量或阻力模型不匹配时跟踪性能易下降。

Method: 在 MPPI 动态模型中纳入低层飞行控制器和电机动态，并采用反映低层控制器行为的迭代混合方案；同时级联 L1 自适应控制，以补偿模型不确定性和外部扰动。

Result: 仿真与实测均显示跟踪性能提升。载荷增加 35% 时，相较普通 MPPI，RMSE 降低 58.61%；相较采用在线质量估计的 MPPI，RMSE 降低 38.59%。实飞速度最高达 13.50 m/s，加速度最高达 2.5 g。

Conclusion: L1-MPPI 能有效增强高速无人机对未知载荷等扰动的鲁棒轨迹跟踪能力，并在实机测试中优于普通 MPPI 及结合在线质量估计的对照方法。

Abstract: This work proposes the L1 Adaptive Model Predictive Path Integral (L1-MPPI). It cascades L1 adaptive control with the Model Predictive Path Integral (MPPI) to improve tracking of high-speed UAV trajectories. Thanks to the L1augmentation, the tracking remains accurate even under model uncertainties and external disturbances, such as an additional payload or a mismatch in the modeled aerodynamic drag. In contrast to existing MPPI approaches for UAV control that do not explicitly model aerodynamic effects, varying payloads, and typically neglect the dynamics of low-level motor controllers, our L1-MPPI approach enhances the dynamic model used in the MPPI by incorporating the low-level flight controller and motor dynamics, as well as an iterative mixing scheme that reflects the approach of the low-level controller. The proposed method demonstrates improved tracking performance in both simulation and the real world, even when the UAV is subjected to an unknown payload. In flight with 35% mass increase, our approach lowers the RMSE by 58.61% with respect to plain MPPI. Compared to the same MPPI using an online mass estimator in place of the L1 augmentation, the RMSE is lower by 38.59%. During the real-world experiments the UAV reaches speeds up to 13.50 m/s and accelerations up to 2.5 g.

</details>


### [80] [Observability Analysis and Online Calibration of Visual-Inertial-Wheel Odometry for 4WIS4WID Mobile Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38462)
*Branimir Ćaran,Vladimir Milić,Bojan Šekoranja,Bojan Jerbić*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出面向四轮独立转向与驱动（4WIS4WID）机器人的视觉—惯性—车轮里程计（VIWO）框架，支持在线标定，并通过仿真和真实机器人实验验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 仅使用驱动信息的里程计模型无法观测所有转向偏置；需要利用四个车轮的冗余运动约束提升标定能力，并明确系统的可观测性及退化条件。

Method: 基于四轮驱动速度和转向角，结合车轮纵向滚动约束与横向无侧滑约束建立二维里程计模型；设计预积分模型和解析雅可比，以提高滤波与标定效率。进一步分析线性化系统的可观测性，并推导轮内参的激励条件。

Result: 分析表明，冗余模型可恢复转向偏置的可观测性；系统还存在四个标准视觉—惯性导航系统的不可观方向，以及与里程计参考系任意选取相关的三个额外不可观方向。研究刻画了零偏航率、转向角恒定和车轮不滚动等退化运动，并给出固定参考系后剩余13个车轮内参的激励条件。仿真与真实机器人实验验证了该方法。

Conclusion: 该VIWO框架结合多轮运动约束与在线标定，可改善4WIS4WID机器人的状态估计和轮内参辨识；可观性与退化分析为传感器标定和运动规划提供了理论依据。

Abstract: In this paper, we present a visual-inertial-wheel odometry (VIWO) framework with online calibration for four-wheel independently steered and driven (4WIS4WID) mobile robots. We derive a 2D odometry model directly from the four driving velocities and steering angles, using both the longitudinal rolling constraints and the lateral no-slip constraints of all wheels. A preintegration model and analytical Jacobians are developed for efficient filtering and calibration. An observability analysis of the linearized VIWO system shows that a drive-only model makes all steering offsets unobservable, whereas the proposed redundant model restores their observability. The analysis also identifies four standard VINS unobservable directions and three additional directions associated with the arbitrary placement of the odometry reference frame. Furthermore, we characterize several degenerate motions, including zero yaw rate, constant steering, and a non-rolling wheel, and derive the corresponding excitation conditions for the thirteen wheel intrinsics that remain after fixing the odometry frame reference. The performance of the proposed system has been demonstrated in both simulation and real-world experiments on a 4WIS4WID mobile robot.

</details>


### [81] [Embodiment-aware control by inference over the operator: a simulation study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38437)
*Sara Falcone*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 研究提出通用具身引擎（UEE），利用隐式行为信号和任务结果推断操作者状态，并据此调整遥操作设备设置；模拟显示其效果优于固定控制和模型无关的老虎机方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 遥操作通常优化信道保真度，而具身感仅在事后通过问卷测量。单纯最小化预测误差可能通过关闭反馈来达成，因此控制器需要同时推断操作者状态，并明确纳入任务偏好。

Method: 将控制问题建模为离散主动推断智能体。UEE根据凝视、瞳孔信号及任务结果，推断操作者的具身感和视觉—本体感觉线索权重，再在偏好约束下选择有界设备设置。研究在300名异质合成操作者的模拟中，将其与固定控制、无操作者感知的自适应控制、模型无关老虎机、期望效用控制及预测误差最小化方法比较。

Result: UEE对多数操作者能在约30秒内找到合适设置，并在会话后半段接近预知操作者类型的预言机表现：具身感得分为1.68，最佳固定设置为0.73（量表范围0—2）。使用相同推断的期望效用控制表现与UEE相同，表明收益来自贝叶斯操作者推断和显式偏好，而非主动推断的信息寻求项。预测误差最小化方法因抑制反馈而降低任务成功率（0.74，对照0.90）。模型外泛化时收益有所减弱但仍存在；在人群多样性增加时收益扩大；信号无信息或线索权重变化未被建模时，收益消失。

Conclusion: 模拟结果支持以操作者推断和明确偏好驱动具身感知控制，但并未证明其在人类使用者中有效。后续人体研究需先验证信号校准，并建立对操作者状态变化的建模能力。

Abstract: Teleoperation systems are tuned for channel fidelity, while whether the operator experiences the device as part of the body, the Sense of Embodiment (SoE), is measured only afterwards, by questionnaire. Predictive-processing accounts suggest controlling devices to reduce the mismatch between the operator's predictions and the returned feedback, but those predictions are unobservable, and an objective that only penalizes mismatch is minimized by removing feedback. We formulate an embodiment-aware controller, the Universal Embodiment Engine (UEE), that infers the operator's embodiment and visuo-proprioceptive cue weighting from implicit gaze and pupil signals and task outcome, and chooses bounded device settings under explicit preferences, cast as a discrete Active Inference agent. In simulations with 300 heterogeneous synthetic operators, the UEE found the suitable setting within half a minute for most operators, before identifying their exact type, and came close to an oracle in the second half of the session (embodiment 1.68 against 0.73 for the best fixed setting, on a 0-2 scale). Adapting without reading the operator did no better than fixed control, and model-free bandits did worse, whereas an expected-utility controller with the same inference did exactly as well: the benefit comes from Bayesian inference over the operator with explicit preferences, not from the information-seeking term of Active Inference. A naive prediction-error minimizer withheld feedback, as its objective implies, and lost task success (0.74 vs 0.90). The benefit shrank but persisted for operators outside the controller's model family, grew with the variety of the population, and vanished when the controller trusted an uninformative signal or when cue weighting changed mid-session without being modeled. These failures show what studies with people must establish first: calibrated signals and a model of change.

</details>


### [82] [A Reachability-based Safety Certificate for Dynamical System Motion Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38421)
*Aditya Vats,Tianyi Xia,Nadia Figueroa*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于价值函数的安全证书，用于评估并保障动力系统（DS）在障碍物环境中的运动安全。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 部署于未知环境时，常用的调制方法和几何控制障碍函数（CBF）可能因障碍物几何形状或系统复杂性而导致碰撞或产生虚假吸引子；传统受控系统的可达性分析还面临维数灾难。

Method: 借鉴后向可达管的思想，构造衡量名义DS沿轨迹运行时最差安全状况的价值函数。由于学习示范场景中的DS没有控制输入，可达性问题可化为确定性轨迹推演；结合稳定性条件，还可将无限时域截断为有限时域。作者进一步证明，该价值函数对应名义DS流在无障碍区域内的最大正向不变子集，并将其用于构建CBF-on-V安全策略。

Result: 该方法在五类DS构造上得到验证，包括解析模型、Neural ODE、可微潜空间模型、LPV-DS和SE(3)系统，并在Franka机械臂上进行验证。针对朝向不安全区域的正面接近情形，作者报告CBF-on-V能够避免调制方法和几何CBF中出现的鞍点问题。

Conclusion: 研究将特定稳定性条件下的确定性DS轨迹分析转化为可计算的安全证书，为DS在未知环境中的安全部署提供了具有不变集保证的方法。

Abstract: Dynamical Systems (DS) are reactive motion policies representing vector fields trained with theoretical guarantees of stability and convergence. To ensure safety during deployment in unknown environments they must be locally reshaped, either through modulation or geometric control barrier function strategies. However, depending on the geometry of the obstacles and the complexity of the DS, these local strategies can lead the system to unavoidable collisions or spurious attractors. In this work, we certify safety with a value function drawn from the notion of backward reachability tube, which measures the worst-case safety along a rollout trajectory of the nominal DS. Usually, such a value function is intractable for a controlled system due to curse of dimensionality. We show that in the DS-based learning-from-demonstration setting, the absence of a control input collapses the reachability problem to a deterministic rollout, and the presence of certain stability conditions truncates the infinite horizon to a finite one, resulting in a well-defined value function. We further show that the value function we devised is the maximal forward-invariant subset of the obstaclefree region for the nominal DS flow. The application of this certificate function is validated across five DS constructions - analytical, Neural ODE, diffeomorphic latent space, LPV-DS, SE(3)and validate it on a Franka manipulator. Modulation and geometric CBFs also suffer from saddle point in cases of headon approach towards an unsafe zone. We show that CBF-on-V avoids this pitfall entirely.

</details>


### [83] [PneuTac: Tactile Manipulation with Soft Pneumatic Robots via Unified MPM-Gaussian Splatting Simulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38418)
*Shaohong Zhong,Marco Pontin,Joe Watson,Perla Maiolino,Ingmar Posner*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: PneuTac 是用于软气动机器人触觉操控的统一仿真框架，结合物理建模、视觉渲染和代理模型生成仿真示范，提升真实任务中的操控策略表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 软机器人与视觉触觉传感器适合精细、接触丰富的操控，但现有仿真通常将两者分开建模，且仿真与真实系统存在较大差距，限制了触觉操控策略的高效训练。

Method: 使用物质点法（MPM）模拟软机器人及可变形触觉膜的动力学，使用3D高斯泼溅（3DGS）进行渲染；通过视觉方法完成实物到仿真的建模，并训练动作与感知代理网络，以高效运行仿真。随后利用该框架构建触觉引导的流程，在仿真中采集示范。

Result: 在自研的带触觉传感器的气动软指及跨设备评估中，PneuTac 能较准确地建模软机器人与触觉传感器。针对三项真实世界、接触丰富的柔顺操控任务，使用仿真增强示范训练的策略优于仅使用相同真实数据训练的基线。

Conclusion: PneuTac 为柔顺硬件上的触觉操控提供了实用的统一仿真与训练方案；实验结果表明，仿真增强示范有助于提升真实任务中的策略表现。

Abstract: Soft robots and tactile sensors have demonstrated great potential in delicate manipulation tasks. Soft pneumatic robots enable safe contact through compliance, and vision-based tactile sensors offer high-resolution touch perception. However, learning tactile manipulation with compliant robots has been challenging, bottlenecked by the lack of efficient simulation. Existing simulators typically model them in isolation, and exhibit large calibration gaps that are difficult to overcome efficiently. We present PneuTac, a unified framework for tactile-feedback manipulation with soft pneumatic robots. We leverage the material point method (MPM) for modelling the dynamics of the soft robot and the deformable tactile membrane, and 3D Gaussian splatting (3DGS) for rendering. Real-to-sim modelling is done with a simple vision-based method, to then train action and perception networks for efficient simulation with surrogate models. We use the framework to drive a tactile-guided pipeline to collect demonstrations in simulation. Through experiments on a custom-designed pneumatic soft finger with a tactile sensing tip, together with additional cross-device evaluations, we show that PneuTac is capable of accurately modelling soft robots with tactile sensors, and that policies trained with simulation-augmented demonstrations outperform baselines trained on the same real data on three real-world contact-rich compliant manipulation tasks, making it a practical framework for tactile manipulation on compliant hardware.

</details>


### [84] [Draft: A Parametric Tool for Robot Design Exploration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38405)
*David Nguyen,Marcelo Coelho,Sangbae Kim*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出Draft，一种无需CAD即可将参数化串联链树编译为可直接仿真的MJCF模型的通用机器人生成工具，并通过真实硬件数据约束设计参数，以支持形态与控制的协同探索。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人性能优化通常需要反复修改形态和控制，但CAD变更必须重新转换为可仿真模型，导致迭代成本高。现有协同设计方法往往针对单一平台开发模型生成器，或缺乏真实世界数据支持，难以保证生成设计的合理性与可实现性。

Method: Draft采用通用参数化生成引擎，将任意由串联链组成的参数化树编译为仿真就绪的MJCF模型。其设计参数趋势基于114个执行器和49个已发表机器人描述进行拟合，从而以真实硬件统计规律约束生成模型。研究进一步构建四种现成机器人的数字孪生模型，并通过两阶段强化学习课程评估三种四足机器人设计。

Result: 四种现成机器人的数字孪生模型在质量预测上达到1.10倍的几何平均折叠误差，说明基于数据拟合的参数趋势能够较好复现实物机器人特征。对三种四足机器人的强化学习评估展示了Draft识别和分析形态设计与控制性能之间权衡的能力。

Conclusion: Draft为机器人形态与控制协同设计提供了平台无关、无需CAD且受真实数据约束的建模工具，可降低设计迭代成本并支持控制性能导向的方案比较。不过，摘要未详细说明误差定义、参数覆盖范围及强化学习实验的具体性能提升，工具的泛化能力仍需在更多机器人平台和真实硬件上验证。

Abstract: Robot performance is often limited by the cost of iterating on morphology and control together, since every computer-aided design (CAD) change has to be carried into a simulation-ready model before control work begins. Co-design methods attempt to close this gap, but each uses a model generator written for a single platform or lack the use of real-world data to suggest that designs are plausible. We present Draft, a parametric generation tool whose generalized engine compiles any parametric tree of serial chains into a simulation-ready MJCF model, without CAD. It allows engineers to explore design tradeoffs through easily adjustable models and evaluate how changes influence controller performance. Draft grounds the free parameters of each design using trends fitted to a survey of $114$ actuators and $49$ published robot descriptions, so that a generated robot is anchored to real-world hardware. We validate those trends wholistically by building twins of four off-the-shelf robots, whose masses agree to $1.10\times$ geometric mean fold error. Finally, we demonstrate how Draft exposes design tradeoffs by evaluating three quadrupeds through a two-stage reinforcement learning curriculum.

</details>


### [85] [Memorize, Adapt, Ignore: Diagnosing Robot Learning Mechanisms under Training Data Variation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38401)
*Ke Zhang,Danica J. Sutherland,Chao Liu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文研究训练数据变化如何影响机器人操作策略的鲁棒性，并利用经验神经切线核（NTK）揭示策略从“记忆具体情形”到“适应当前情形”的内部学习机制转变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 域随机化和示范数据设计是提升机器人策略泛化能力的关键手段，但随机化参数通常依赖昂贵的试错，且其作用机制尚不清晰。本文旨在建立可解释的诊断信号，为随机化方案设计、模型选择和捷径学习检测提供依据。

Method: 在ManiSkill中的抓取放置强化学习，以及LIBERO和RoboTwin上的视觉语言动作（VLA）模型微调中，系统随机化物体大小、颜色、类别、场景光照和语言提示。研究结合策略行为与内部表示分析，以经验NTK为主要诊断工具，并使用NTK信噪比评估模型是否忽略任务无关因素。研究还比较不同表示形式，并通过基于ACT的模仿学习真实机器人实验进行验证。

Result: 结果表明，NTK能够区分两种学习机制：数据变化不足时，策略分别记忆不同情形；变化充分时，策略能够根据当前情境进行适应。NTK信噪比还能识别策略是否真正忽略无关因素，例如对不同颜色的物体采用相同策略，而不是分别学习颜色特定的子策略。相关诊断在多种任务、模型和表示形式中具有一致性，并得到真实硬件实验支持。

Conclusion: 训练数据变化不仅影响策略性能，还会改变其内部学习机制。适当的域随机化和示范数据设计有助于形成基于任务相关因素的适应性策略，减少情境记忆和捷径学习。经验NTK及其信噪比可作为实用工具，用于指导随机化参数选择、评估模型泛化机制并诊断机器人策略的鲁棒性。

Abstract: Training data variation, whether through designing a domain randomization (DR) scheme in simulation or curating demonstrations for imitation learning, is a primary lever for improving the robustness of robotic manipulation policies. Yet its underlying mechanisms remain poorly understood, and practitioners typically select randomization parameters through expensive trial and error. We investigate these mechanisms through a series of case studies, randomizing object size, color, and type as well as scene lighting and linguistic prompts across settings including pick-and-place RL in ManiSkill and fine-tuning of vision-language-action (VLA) models on LIBERO and RoboTwin. We examine both model behavior and internal representations, using the empirical neural tangent kernel (NTK) as our primary diagnostic tool. We show that the NTK distinguishes a shift in the internal learning mechanism from \textit{memorizing} different situations with insufficient variation (e.g.\ learning what to do for a large cube, and what to do for a small cube) to \textit{adapting} to the situation at hand with sufficient variation. An NTK-based signal-to-noise ratio also helps distinguish when policies have learned to \emph{ignore} task-irrelevant factors (e.g.\ treating blue and red cubes identically, instead of learning a blue sub-policy and a red sub-policy). We use these diagnostics to develop practical guidance for designing DR schemes, selecting models, and detecting shortcut learning. We further compare different kinds of representations and validate our findings with real-world hardware experiments using ACT-based imitation learning.

</details>


### [86] [TALK-Dem: Benchmarking Embodied Task Planning under Dementia-Associated Communication Patterns](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38371)
*Guangxin Zhao,Yiran Hu,Yuan Cao,Chenxi Jiang,Jianfei Yang,Yegang Du,Yasuyuki Taki,Yoshifumi Kitamura,Lin Gu,Zhi Zheng*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 论文提出 TALK-Dem 基准，用于评估大语言模型驱动的机器人任务规划在与痴呆相关的非典型口语交流中的鲁棒性，并提出 CARE 方法提升规划表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器人任务规划器通常假设用户指令清晰、完整且聚焦任务；面对真实交流中的含糊、偏题或信息缺失时，模型可能规划失误并带来安全风险。对于受隐私和网络条件限制、需要本地部署的辅助机器人，这一问题尤为重要。

Method: 构建 TALK-Dem，包含 4,800 条指令，覆盖五种交流模式——指称不精确、物体替代、空洞言语、话题漂移和言语侵入，并设置三个强度等级。评估六种开放权重模型；同时提出 CARE，通过检索先前已解决的相关任务，为当前指令提供任务特定的解释与规划上下文。

Result: 六种模型在非理想交流模式下均显示鲁棒性缺口，相较理想指令，性能最高下降 22.3 个百分点。CARE 整体优于标准提示基线，相比直接使用基础提示，平均任务成功率提升 18.1 个百分点。

Conclusion: 研究表明，评估真实交流条件下的鲁棒性对辅助机器人至关重要；基于经验检索的上下文增强可有效改善本地部署模型的任务规划表现。

Abstract: Existing LLM-driven robot task planners rely on a taken-for-granted assumption of an ideal user whose instructions are clear, complete, and task-focused. However, when interacting with real-world users, especially those experiencing cognitive impairments, such as people living with dementia (PLWD), the planners often make mistakes and even pose physical safety risks. We proposed TALK-Dem (Talking Attributes and Linguistic Knowledge in Dementia), the first benchmark for evaluating LLM-driven robot task planning under dementia-associated verbal communication. TALK-Dem contains 4,800 instructions and covers five typical communication patterns, including Referential Imprecision, Object Substitution, Empty Speech, Topic Drift, and Intrusion, at three intensity levels. Experiments across six open-weight LLMs reveal a substantial robustness gap. Across communication patterns, open-weight models exhibited performance drops of up to 22.3 percentage points compared to ideal instructions. This revealed a critical gap and even danger for real-world applications, especially in assistive robotics, where locally deployable models are necessary due to privacy concerns and connectivity constraints. To mitigate this issue, we proposed the Context-Aware Retrieval from Experience (CARE) method, which retrieves relevant previously resolved tasks to provide task-specific interpretation and planning context. CARE generally outperformed standard prompting baselines across the six open-weight models, improving average task success by 18.1 percentage points over the vanilla prompt. These results highlighted the importance of both evaluating communication robustness and developing effective adaptation strategies for locally deployable assistive robots. The TALK-Dem dataset is publicly available at this https URL .

</details>


### [87] [A Two-Echelon Covering Tour Vehicle Routing Problem with Drones for Post-Disaster Relief](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38227)
*Dang Viet Anh Nguyen,Rajesh Piplani,Aldy Gunawan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出两级覆盖式巡回车辆路径问题（2E-CTVRP），研究卡车与无人机协同配送，并设计混合元启发式求解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 灾后配送需要统筹卡车运输、无人机投送、卫星站分配及受灾地点集群分配，以降低整体到达时间。

Method: 建立混合整数线性规划模型，并提出GRASP-ILS-PR算法，结合贪婪随机构造、单轨迹搜索和周期性路径重连；在110个新基准实例上进行测试，并比较不同算法与车队配置。

Result: GRASP-ILS-PR在30秒内找到所有已知最优解；对多数较大实例，其结果匹配或优于商业求解器运行一小时所得结果，在含50个卫星站的实例中最多改善11%。该算法优于采用进化式路径重连的传统GRASP，且比使用相同搜索组件的模拟退火算法结果更稳定。

Conclusion: 多数实例中，少量大型卡车更能降低累计到达时间；而较多小型卡车在所有实例中都能更早、更均衡地向受灾地点配送物资。

Abstract: We introduce the two-echelon covering tour vehicle routing problem (2E-CTVRP) for the distribution of relief supplies after a disaster. In the first echelon, a fleet of trucks transports supplies and drones from a central depot to satellites at the periphery of the affected area. In the second echelon, drones launched in parallel from the satellites deliver the supplies to the centroids of victim clusters, which are obtained by clustering the victim locations, and each truck waits at a satellite until its drones have returned. The problem combines the assignment of satellites to trucks, the sequencing of the truck routes, and the assignment of clusters to satellites, and minimizes the sum of the arrival times of the trucks at the satellites and at the depot. We formulate the 2E-CTVRP as a mixed integer linear program and propose a hybrid metaheuristic, GRASP-ILS-PR, which combines greedy randomized construction, a single-trajectory search, and periodic path relinking on the assignment of clusters to satellites. On a new benchmark set of 110 instances, GRASP-ILS-PR finds all known optimal solutions within 30 seconds, matches or improves on the one-hour solutions of a commercial solver on most larger instances, and improves on them by up to 11% for instances with 50 satellites. It clearly outperforms a conventional GRASP with evolutionary path relinking and yields more consistent results than a simulated annealing algorithm that uses the same search components. An analysis of two fleet configurations shows that few large trucks minimize the cumulative arrival time in most instances, whereas many small trucks deliver supplies to the victims earlier and more equitably in every instance.

</details>


### [88] [Fiatlux: A Long-Horizon Benchmark for Humanoid Ladder Climbing and Light-Bulb Replacement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38216)
*Pavel Bushuyeu,Yujin Chen,Anton Nikolaev,Brian Shu,Igor Molybog*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Fiatlux 是面向人形机器人的长时程基准：机器人需搬放梯子、攀爬并更换灯泡，同时妥善处置旧灯泡，且不得损坏或跌落灯泡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准通常将桌面操作、家庭活动与人形机器人移动和操作分开评估，未能同时考查垂直移动、灵巧操作及易碎物体处理。

Method: 基于 NVIDIA Isaac Lab 构建灯泡更换任务，将完整流程拆分为 12 个子任务，并以难度加权门槛评分；提供标准观测与特权观测模式、评估协议，以及 RSL-RL PPO、零样本 NVIDIA GR00T N1.7 VLA 和全身控制器基线，并公开用于制定和核验成功判据的遥操作记录。

Result: 基准定义了完整成功条件：新灯泡安装到位、旧灯泡进入处置箱、两者均未掉落，且灯泡未超过易碎性损伤界限；未完成全部目标的运行仍可获得部分得分。摘要未报告基线的量化实验结果。

Conclusion: Fiatlux 为评估人形机器人在单次长时程任务中结合攀爬、全身协调与精细易碎物体操作提供了统一基准和配套资源。

Abstract: Existing benchmarks evaluate tabletop manipulation, flat-floor household activity, or humanoid locomotion and manipulation as separate task groups; none scores vertical mobility and dexterous work on a fragile payload in one long-horizon episode. We present Fiatlux, a light-bulb replacement benchmark built on NVIDIA Isaac Lab. In one episode, a Unitree G1 humanoid positions a step ladder under a ceiling or wall fixture, climbs it, exchanges a spent bulb in a socket for a fresh one, and leaves the spent one in a disposal crate. We decompose the episode into twelve subtask environments scored on difficulty-weighted gates. The goal is a successful replacement, with the fresh bulb seated, the spent one disposed of, neither dropped, and a fragility bound not crossed. Runs that fall short can earn partial credit. Observations are split into a standard mode (signals a physical robot could sense or estimate) and a privileged mode (exact simulator state). We specify the evaluation protocol and provide reference baseline implementations spanning RSL-RL PPO, zero-shot NVIDIA GR00T N1.7 Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models, and whole-body controllers. Additionally, we provide the teleoperated recordings used to specify and check the success gates. The benchmark code and the teleoperated recordings are available at this http URL .

</details>


### [89] [Optimize, Learn, Refine: Whole-Body Grasping and Pick-and-Throw with a Spiral Soft Robot](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38202)
*Marwah Basuhai,Tingcong Liu,Ibrahim Alsarraj,Yuhao Wang,Ke Wu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种面向软体连续机器人全身抓取与抓取后抛掷的优化框架，在仿真和硬件实验中均取得较高成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 软体连续机器人可利用分布式柔顺性进行全身操作，但在接触状态不断变化时，难以直接合成可靠行为。

Method: 以抓取结果为目标进行驱动空间优化：用末端角度扫掠和机身包围程度衡量抓取；抛掷任务还考虑释放方向对齐与最低释放速度。针对驱动到结果映射的非光滑特性，采用无导数 CMA-ES，并构建“优化—学习—细化”流程：先为采样条件生成解，再训练任务条件预测器提供热启动，最后针对未见条件进行细化。

Result: 仿真中抓取成功492/500次（98.4%），三个方向的抛掷成功率分别为98%、97%和94%。学习式初始化将抓取成功率从78.6%提升至98.4%，并将CMA-ES中位迭代次数从1184降至816。硬件实验中，50次抓取成功率为100%；30次抓取并抛掷实验（每个方向重复10次）成功率也为100%。

Conclusion: 结果表明，该框架无需预先指定接触力、接触位置或机身构型，即可利用柔顺接触实现全身抓取与抛掷，并在仿真和实体机器人上表现出良好效果。

Abstract: Soft continuum robots can exploit distributed compliance for whole-body manipulation, but synthesizing behavior through changing contacts remains difficult. We address whole-body grasping and pick-and-throw from an initially ungrasped state through outcome-based actuation-space optimization. Grasping is quantified by tip angular sweep and body-object enclosure, while throwing further incorporates release-direction alignment and minimum release speed. These objectives allow grasping, acceleration, and release to emerge from compliant interaction without prescribing contact forces, contact locations, or body configurations. Because the resulting actuation-to-outcome mapping is nonsmooth, we utilize derivative-free CMA-ES within an optimize-learn-refine framework. CMA-ES generates solutions for sampled conditions, a task-conditioned predictor learns warm starts, and CMA-ES refines them for unseen conditions. In simulation, the method achieves 492/500 successful grasps (98.4%) and success rates of 98%, 97%, and 94% across three directional throwing trials. Learned initialization increases grasping success from 78.6% to 98.4% while reducing the median rollout count from 1184 to 816 in CMA-ES. Hardware experiments achieve a 100% grasping success rate across 50 executions and a 100% pick-and-throw success rate across 30 executions, with 10 repetitions per direction. Together, these simulation and hardware results demonstrate the effectiveness of the proposed framework across both simulated and physical whole-body manipulation tasks.

</details>


### [90] [TACTIC: Temporal and Context-Aware LLM Tactical Planning for Roadside LiDAR Attacks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39969)
*Yiming Gao,Shaocheng Luo*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 此内容未通过合规测试，已隐藏。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此内容未通过合规测试，已隐藏。

Method: 此内容未通过合规测试，已隐藏。

Result: 此内容未通过合规测试，已隐藏。

Conclusion: 此内容未通过合规测试，已隐藏。

Abstract: Physical LiDAR attacks are often evaluated using fixed primitives and manually selected parameters, despite their strong dependence on surrounding traffic. We present TACTIC, a scene-aware framework that uses a multimodal large language model (MLLM) to coordinate state-adaptive roadside LiDAR attacks. Under a gray-box threat model, TACTIC relies only on an attacker-operated roadside perception stack, without accessing the victim LiDAR's native point clouds or internal processing. Local perception provides metric vehicle states, while the MLLM combines these measurements with roadside imagery to infer relational traffic context and construct a semantic scene graph. Based on this representation, TACTIC selects and configures two complementary primitives: \emph{push-away}, which shifts the perceived range of a lead vehicle, and \emph{phantom-obstacle braking}, which triggers emergency braking through obstacle injection. Measured traffic states and empirically calibrated constraints ground the generated tactics in physically feasible operating regions. To accommodate MLLM latency, TACTIC overlaps reasoning and execution asynchronously while high-rate local perception detects scene changes and triggers replanning. Across 280 randomized CARLA trials, the full policy achieves a 100% collision rate, compared with 35% for a fixed rule, 60% for random selection, and 75% for a restricted LLM using mode selection with default parameters. Joint physical-and-image input achieves 100% success, versus 65% with physical measurements alone and 75% with imagery alone, while asynchronous $\Delta$ refresh reduces scene-mutation response from 7.4 s to 2.0 s. These results show that scene-dependent tactical planning can expose context-sensitive LiDAR failure modes that fixed attack policies may miss.

</details>


### [91] [Learning from Runtime Feedback through Failure-Bank Self-Evolution for Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39820)
*Mingyue Cui,Zheyuan Liu,Yihan Zhu,Zheyuan Zhang,Meng Jiang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: FailBank 将运行时安全反馈转化为持续的策略改进，在提升任务成功率的同时降低策略诱发的累计成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 运行时安全屏蔽虽能修正单步动作，却不改变底层策略；反复分歧可能形成持续的策略—屏蔽不匹配，阻碍任务完成。

Method: 提出四阶段自进化框架 FailBank：固定的基于控制屏障函数（CBF）的安全模块以仅观察方式提供反事实修正；随后依据结果筛选有用建议，将其转为纠正目标，并保留成功且未经修正的动作为稳定锚点，用于受约束的 LoRA 更新。

Result: 在 VLA-Arena 的两个难度级别和两种 VLA 骨干上，相较基础策略，任务成功率分别提升 8.5 和 6.9 个百分点，策略诱发的累计成本分别降低 35.6% 和 23.8%。相较运行时屏蔽，成功率分别提高 25.4 和 9.5 个百分点，同时保持相近的策略诱发累计成本。

Conclusion: 运行时反馈不仅可作为临时动作约束，也可转化为持久的策略监督，从而改善成功率与成本之间的权衡。

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) models generalize broadly across robotic manipulation tasks, but complex environments require balancing task success with unintended contact. Runtime shields can correct individual actions, but they leave the underlying policy unchanged, so repeated disagreements may create a persistent policy-shield mismatch that blocks task progress. To address this challenge, we introduce FailBank, a four-stage self-evolving framework that converts runtime feedback into persistent policy improvement. During collection, a fixed CBF-based safety module serves as an observe-only teacher, producing counterfactual corrections while the policy remains in control. Outcome-aware admission then converts useful proposals into corrective targets and retains successful uncorrected actions as quiet anchors for guarded LoRA updates. We evaluate FailBank on the VLA-Arena benchmark across two difficulty levels and two VLA backbones. Compared with the base policies, FailBank improves the joint success-cost operating point. Across the two backbones, FailBank improves task success rate by 8.5 and 6.9 percentage points, while reducing policy-induced cumulative cost by 35.6\% and 23.8\%, respectively. Compared with runtime shielding, FailBank raises task success rate by 25.4 and 9.5 percentage points, while maintaining comparable policy-induced cumulative cost. These results show that runtime feedback can serve as persistent policy supervision rather than only as a temporary action constraint.

</details>


### [92] [DiffWAM: A Fast and Efficient Navigation World Action Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39763)
*Mo Zhu,Yuze Wu,Xijie Huang,Xiao Cui,Fei Gao,Xin Zhou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出 DiffWAM，将冻结视频模型的预测特征直接转换为连续三维相机轨迹，并以 FastDreamer 支持无人机实时、异步执行。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法通常需要生成未来视频并进行几何重建，计算开销较大。本文探究能否从视频模型的预测表征中直接提取隐含运动，减少部署阶段的计算负担。

Method: DiffWAM 使用 Grid-Motion 模块保留时空运动关联，并通过 Latent2Pose 将预测特征与首帧几何信息结合，恢复具有度量尺度的三维运动。完整视频生成与几何重建仅用于离线监督。FastDreamer 将预测和几何计算与飞行过程重叠，并采用时间戳感知的异步轨迹交接。

Result: 在 DiffWAM-1000（1,000 个样本）上，轨迹 RMSE 为 0.3492 米，终点成功率为 74.40%。真实场景实验展示了受限空间穿越、环绕、S 形飞行及多阶段导航等行为；Jetson AGX Thor 上的 DiffWAM-Flash 模型流水线延迟为 1.08 秒。

Conclusion: 实验表明，视频预测表征能够直接映射为连续三维运动，为“先生成、再重建”的导航流程提供了更高效的替代方案。

Abstract: Pretrained video foundation models encode rich semantic and spatiotemporal priors for embodied navigation, yet converting these priors into UAV motion typically requires expensive future-video synthesis and geometric reconstruction. We investigate whether the motion implicit in future visual prediction can instead be recovered directly from the predictive representations of a frozen video model. To this end, we present DiffWAM, a geometry-conditioned navigation world-action model that directly transforms multi-level predictive features into continuous camera trajectories. Its Grid-Motion module preserves spatial-temporal motion associations, while Latent2Pose grounds them with first-frame geometry to recover metrically meaningful 3D motion. Complete video rollouts and geometric reconstruction are required only for offline supervision, eliminating future-video decoding and multi-frame reconstruction during deployment. We further introduce FastDreamer, which overlaps predictive and geometric computation with ongoing flight and performs timestamp-aware asynchronous trajectory handoff for continuous UAV execution. DiffWAM achieves a trajectory RMSE of 0.3492 m and an endpoint success rate of 74.40% on the 1,000-sample DiffWAM-1000 benchmark, while representative real-world experiments demonstrate complex behaviors including constrained traversal, orbiting, S-shaped flight, and multi-stage navigation. An onboard DiffWAM-Flash implementation further reaches 1.08 s model-pipeline latency on NVIDIA Jetson AGX Thor. These results demonstrate that predictive video representations can be efficiently grounded into continuous 3D motion, providing a direct alternative to generate-then-reconstruct navigation pipelines. Project page: this https URL .

</details>


### [93] [RoboCoach: World Models as Active Coaches for Compositional Robot Skills](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39685)
*Jiajun Liu,Yifan Chen,Yichao Liu,Jiayi Zhang,Ruoqu Chen,Shaoxuan Xie,Guocai Yao,Mengdi Xu,Sen Cui,Changshui Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: ROBOCOACH利用世界模型模拟任务失败，定向选择示范数据和技能专家进行更新，从而提升长时程机器人操作表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长时程任务需要组合并复用多种技能，但通过端到端示范改进任务组合成本高昂。系统需要判断下一步应收集哪类示范，以及应将监督用于哪个技能专家。

Method: 提出ROBOCOACH及其RIDI循环：在共享的动作条件世界模型COACHWORLD中执行可复用技能专家，由进度评估器记录最先未完成的子任务；汇总失败记录后，选择需要补充示范的子任务，并更新对应专家适配器。

Result: 在两套仿真环境和两个真实机器人平台上，22组任务与策略的模拟成功率和实际成功率相关系数为0.840。受控比较显示，该方法在相同数据预算和更新计划下优于基线。额外收集150条子任务示范后，Franka成功率从13.3%升至75.0%，AgileX从40.0%升至83.8%。在四种留出的任务组合上，迁移平均成功率为35.0%，而使用均匀采集示范更新的共享策略基线为0%。

Conclusion: 世界模型可以充当主动教练，将模拟失败转化为针对性的示范采集与模块化策略改进信号，从而以有限数据提升技能表现并促进跨任务组合迁移。

Abstract: Long-horizon robot manipulation reuses skills across many task compositions, but improving these compositions with additional end-to-end demonstrations is costly. A practical self-improving system must decide both what to teach next and where to apply that supervision. We present ROBOCOACH, a world-model-guided coaching framework that uses imagined failures to guide demonstration requests and expert updates. Its Route-Imagine-Diagnose-Improve (RIDI) loop executes reusable skill experts inside COACHWORLD, our shared action-conditioned world model, and uses a progress judge to record the first subtask that fails to complete. Aggregated records select which subtask demonstrations to acquire and which expert adapters to update. Across two simulation suites and two real-robot platforms, imagined and deployed success correlate over 22 task-policy pairs (rho = 0.840). Controlled comparisons show that our coaching method outperforms matched baselines under matched data budgets and update schedules. With only 150 additional subtask demonstrations, success rises from 13.3% to 75.0% on Franka and from 40.0% to 83.8% on AgileX. The coached experts also transfer to four held-out compositions, achieving an average success of 35.0%, compared with 0% for a shared-policy baseline updated with uniformly acquired demonstrations. Together, these results show that world models can serve as active coaches, turning imagined failures into targeted supervision for modular policy improvement. Project Page: this https URL

</details>


### [94] [Text-to-3D Policy: Fine-Grained Language-Behavior Alignment for Unseen Specification Generalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39599)
*Xinhao Yang,Wenhao Wu,Ning Lv,Yanshen Ding,Zhenhong Sun,Daoyi Dong,Chunlin Chen,Zhi Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: T3DP通过语言与行为片段的双向逐词对应，提升3D操作策略对未见细粒度行为指令的泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文本到3D策略通常依赖全局语言—行为对齐，虽能理解粗略任务语义，却容易混淆目标位置、位移或关节状态等相近但行为不同的指令，导致难以泛化到训练示范未覆盖的具体规格。

Method: T3DP保留指令和示范的局部结构，建立语言元素与行为片段间的双向逐词对应，以形成对行为规格敏感的语言表示，并将其用于点云条件的3D扩散策略；无需更改底层策略架构。

Result: 在Meta-World、ManiSkill和RoboTwin上，相较全局语言—行为对齐，留出规格的平均成功率提高11.0至14.2个百分点，且15类任务均有提升。真实机器人任务的平均成功率从47.5%提高至65.0%，提升17.5个百分点。表示与动作探针分析也显示，细粒度对齐更能保留规格结构及与动作相关的变化。

Conclusion: 将语言指令与其影响的局部行为成分显式对应，有助于避免相近规格在表示空间中混为一谈，从而改善未见细粒度行为规格下的控制表现。

Abstract: 3D visuomotor policies provide a strong foundation for spatially precise manipulation, yet current text-to-3D policies struggle to follow unseen fine-grained behavioral specifications beyond those covered by demonstrations. We study this challenge as unseen specification generalization, where language specifies behaviorally significant variations, such as target position, displacement, or articulated state, that are absent from policy training. We find that pretrained language representations and conventional global behavior-language alignment capture coarse task semantics but often blur nearby specifications that require distinct behaviors. We introduce T3DP, a Text-to-3D Policy framework for fine-grained language-behavior alignment. Rather than compressing each instruction and demonstration into a single global embedding, T3DP preserves their local structures and establishes bidirectional token-level correspondence between linguistic elements and behavioral segments. This directly grounds subtle linguistic variations in the behavior components they affect, preventing closely related specifications from collapsing in the representation space. The resulting specification-sensitive language representation conditions a point-cloud-based 3D diffusion policy, enabling more precise control over unseen behavioral specifications without modifying the underlying policy architecture. Across Meta-World, ManiSkill, and RoboTwin, T3DP improves average held-out-specification success over global language-behavior alignment by +11.0-14.2 points, with gains on all 15 task families; on real-robot tasks, it further raises average success from 47.5% to 65.0% (+17.5 points). Representation and action-probe analyses show that fine-grained alignment better preserves specification geometry and action-relevant variation, linking local behavior grounding to downstream control.

</details>


### [95] [ECHO-G: Embodied Co-speech Humanoid mOtion Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39575)
*Yizhao Li,Pusen Gao,Ming Wang,Shaojie Shen,Shuo Yang,Hao Xu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: ECHO-G 根据语音音频与带时间戳的文本，为人形机器人生成全身伴随语音动作，并在实体机器人上进行了演示。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人伴随语音动作生成需要协调语音韵律、语言内容与机器人自身的身体结构；现有的人体动作生成后再重定向的流程可能不适合直接生成机器人动作。

Method: 提出 ECHO-G 框架及语音驱动扩散 Transformer（SGDiT），分别处理帧级声学特征和词元级语言信息，并通过整流流匹配直接在机器人动作空间建模。研究还构建了源自 BEAT2 的音频—文本—机器人数据集与评测基准。

Result: 对比评测显示，在所比较的方法中，直接在机器人动作空间生成优于人体动作生成后重定向；消融实验和视频评分研究支持音频与文本联合条件输入的优势。系统还在实体人形机器人上完成演示。

Conclusion: 研究表明，联合利用音频与文本、并直接面向机器人动作空间生成，是生成机器人伴随语音动作的一种有效方案；作者同时提供数据集及训练、推理和评测代码。

Abstract: Generating full-body co-speech motion for humanoid robots requires coordinating speech prosody, linguistic content, and embodiment-specific motion. To this end, we present ECHO-G, a framework jointly conditioned on speech audio and timed transcripts. Its Speech-Grounded Diffusion Transformer (SGDiT) combines frame-aligned acoustic features with token-level linguistic context, preserving their distinct granularities. Trained with rectified flow matching, it models one-to-many utterance-motion relationships directly in robot space. To support training and evaluation, we introduce a BEAT2-derived audio-text-robot dataset and a benchmark covering co-speech characteristics, robot-motion quality, and runtime efficiency. Comparative evaluation supports direct robot-space generation over the evaluated human-motion generation and retargeting pipelines, while modality ablations highlight the benefits of joint audio-text conditioning. We further demonstrate deployment on a physical humanoid robot. A complementary video-rating study also favors joint conditioning over the alternatives. The dataset and training, inference, and evaluation code are available through our project page.

</details>


### [96] [HiWE: Hierarchical World Knowledge Model with Visual Keypoint Enhancement for Zero-Shot 3D Path Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39323)
*Guoqing Ma,Mingqi Yuan,Chen Gao,Jiayu Chen,Shan Yu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: HiWE 将视觉定位、语言规划与抓取执行连接起来，使机器人能够依据指令完成操作任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人示范生成需要协同解决交互位置识别、可行运动规划和接触执行问题；该研究旨在通过统一接口衔接视觉理解与语言规划。

Method: PointVLM 根据图像预测与任务相关的物体坐标，并利用点标注、分割采样、机器人观测和视觉问答数据进行指令微调；深度信息将预测提升为语义三维表示。3DLLM 据此生成末端执行器路径点和夹爪指令，混合抓取模块进一步确定局部抓取姿态。

Result: 评估涵盖14项仿真操作任务和4项实体机器人任务，并通过消融实验考察视觉训练数据、空间输入及抓取选择的作用。摘要未报告具体性能数值。文中所称零样本执行是指部署时无需针对任务进行示范训练，但视觉模型微调使用了既有机器人数据。

Conclusion: HiWE 提出以点为基础的视觉定位与语言规划协同框架。该文介绍其原始形式，并在引言中说明了它与后续 GeneralVLA 扩展的关系。

Abstract: Robot demonstration generation requires a system to identify where an interaction should occur, plan a feasible motion, and execute the required contact. HiWE connects these decisions through a point-based interface between visual grounding and language-based planning. PointVLM is instruction-tuned to associate task-relevant objects with image coordinates using a mixture of point annotations, segmentation-derived samples, robot observations, and visual question answering data. Depth measurements lift these predictions into a semantic 3D representation. A language planner, 3DLLM, uses this representation to specify end-effector waypoints and gripper commands, while a hybrid grasping module resolves local grasp poses. The evaluation covers 14 simulated manipulation tasks and four physical-robot tasks, together with ablations of the visual training data, spatial inputs, and grasp selection. Here, zero-shot execution refers to deployment without task-specific demonstration training; the visual model uses existing robot data during fine-tuning. This paper describes the original point-based formulation of the framework; its relationship to the subsequent GeneralVLA extension is detailed in the introduction.

</details>


### [97] [Scale and Selection: What Makes Automatic Harness Evolution Work for Visual-Interface Robot Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39304)
*Zhijie Wei,Ferris Tan,Jinghui Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 研究表明，编码智能体可自动改进视觉界面机器人智能体的执行框架，但效果取决于每轮评估的轨迹数量和修订筛选机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人智能体的表现很大程度上受提示、工具及控制规则组成的执行框架影响，而该框架此前主要依靠人工编写。研究旨在验证能否由另一编码智能体自动优化这一框架，并明确优化成功的关键条件。

Method: 使用优化智能体迭代修改执行框架，通过多轮任务轨迹评估修订效果；考察每轮训练轨迹数量对泛化的影响，并比较无条件采纳修订与冠军—挑战者筛选机制，后者仅在新版本于同一固定评估集上严格优于现有版本时才予以采纳。

Result: 将训练轨迹从5条增至100条后，留出任务成功率由47%升至67%；小规模训练集出现过拟合，训练成功率达70%，留出成功率却仅为54%。无条件采纳修订会导致性能在十轮内下滑；采用冠军—挑战者筛选后，留出成功率在30轮内由51%升至67%。

Conclusion: 自动进化视觉界面机器人智能体的执行框架具有可行性。可靠增益依赖充足的评估轨迹，以提高修订判断的信噪比，并依赖严格的版本筛选，避免不当修改累积。

Abstract: When an off-the-shelf coding agent is used directly as a robot policy, observing a browser-based 3D interface through screenshots and acting by posing a virtual target gripper through a few tools, the agent's harness, its prompts, tools, and control rules, largely determines success, and until now it has been written by hand. We show that this harness can be improved automatically by another coding agent, the optimizer agent, and report two findings about what makes it work. First, the number of rollouts the optimizer agent sees per round governs whether the evolved harness is trustworthy, generalizes, and improves steadily. A single rollout is a noisy binary outcome, so with few rollouts per round a revision can be promoted on luck; enlarging the batch raises the signal-to-noise ratio of every promotion decision. Holding rounds fixed and growing the training set from 5 to 100 rollouts, held-out success rises from 47% to 67%, while small training sets overfit, reaching 70% on training tasks but only 54% held-out. Second, the optimizer agent must not be given free rein. With every revision it proposes accepted unconditionally, performance drifts downward within ten rounds as ill-judged edits accumulate; adding the most basic safeguard, Champion-Challenger selection that promotes a revision only if it strictly beats the incumbent on the same fixed evaluation set, turns the same loop into one that raises held-out success from 51% to 67% over 30 rounds. Automatic harness evolution for visual-interface robot agents is thus feasible, but its gains hinge on the rollout scale behind each decision and on how the optimizer agent's revisions are selected.

</details>


### [98] [Blackout vs. Freeze: Analyzing Physical Failure Modes of VLAs under Camera Faults](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39145)
*Heejae Suh,Jongwook Han,Zahra Gholami,Yohan Jo*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 研究发现，视觉输入故障会以不同方式影响 VLA 模型的任务表现与物理行为；缓解措施虽能在部分条件下提升成功率，也可能增加非预期接触或环境扰动。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉语言动作模型依赖相机输入，图像黑屏或冻结可能导致任务失败及不安全的机器人行为，因此需要厘清故障类型、可用信息和缓解方法的影响。

Method: 测试 π0.5 和 GR00T 在图像黑屏、画面冻结等输入故障下的表现；通过选择性干预评估本体感觉和不同视角信息的作用，并比较相机黑屏训练与无需训练的故障视觉嵌入替换，还开展真实机器人实验。

Result: 黑屏与冻结即使造成相近的任务成功率，也会引发不同失效行为：冻结更易导致关节动作幅度极端；夹爪闭合后发生黑屏时，更易出现物体掉落，且缺少本体感觉时尤为明显。本体感觉可部分弥补机器人外观信息缺失并减少非目标接触，但无法充分弥补腕部视角中物体信息的缺失。两种缓解方法在部分条件下提高任务成功率，却也可能增加非预期接触或周边物体扰动；真实机器人实验同样观察到成功执行伴随非预期物理交互。

Conclusion: VLA 策略应在相机故障时充分利用仍可用的机器人状态与物体信息，并以限制危险动作和非预期交互为目标，而不能只依据任务成功率评价鲁棒性。

Abstract: Unreliable visual inputs can harm task performance and cause potential physical safety risks for vision-language-action (VLA) models. We analyze how $\pi 0.5$ and GR00T models act under input faults such as image blackouts and freezing. We find that blackout and freezing produce distinct physical failure modes even when task-success rates are similarly low: freezing causes more extreme joint behavior, whereas blackout after gripper closure can cause more object drops, most markedly without proprioception. Selective intervention studies reveal that proprioception (current robot state) partly compensates for the removed robot depictions and reduces non-target contact. However, it cannot sufficiently restore task success when wrist-view object information is removed, even when aided by the remaining scene view. We then evaluate two mitigation approaches: camera-blackout training and training-free replacement of faulty visual embeddings. Both improve task success in selected conditions, but can increase unintended contact or disturbance to surrounding objects. Real-robot trials further show that successful execution under camera faults can still involve unintended physical interactions. These findings motivate designing VLA policies that use the robot and object information still available under camera faults to limit hazardous motion.

</details>


### [99] [Make Code as Policy Great Again: Frontier Agents Write, Call, and Evolve Robot Tools](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39018)
*Shijia Ge,Alex Zhou,Jianshu Zeng,Yexing Wan,Di Wu,Zelin Zheng,Yazhe Wang,Zhiqi Jia,Xuan Shangguan,Jay Zhu,Yijun Liu,Lingyu He,Sihang Wu,Xiao He,Hongcheng Gao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: URAI 将机器人操作拆分为可复用工具构建、工具执行和模型逐步决策，在仿真及真实机器人任务中提升成功率，并在多数测试智能体上减少耗时与输出 token。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 直接让模型逐步规划每个伸手、抓取和撤回动作，推理成本高且执行缓慢；而将后续决策完全交给预先生成的程序，又会削弱模型根据新观察灵活调整的能力。

Method: URAI由编程智能体和执行智能体组成。编程智能体根据任务意图创建并通过执行反馈及人工指导改进通用或任务专用工具；执行智能体根据当前观察选择并参数化工具，每次调用在本地完成一段完整动作后返回控制权。工具版本可跨任务保留，无需更新基础模型权重，并可通过共享 GUI 和 API 供人类及智能体使用。

Result: 在五项 RoboDojo 任务和四个冻结执行智能体上，相比直接指尖控制，URAI 的总体成功率从18.0%升至53.0%，Swap Blocks任务提升最大。使用相同工具时，允许两个智能体在每次调用后重新决策的成功率为56%，高于预先编写程序的24%。四个智能体中有三个完成任务的速度提高至1.3—1.5倍，执行智能体输出 token 减少至原来的约1/1.5—1/1.7；DeepSeek-V4-Flash的成本变化较小。论文还在七项真实 AgileX 双臂任务上进行了评估，涵盖物体操作、叠衣和人机互动井字棋。

Conclusion: URAI以可复用、可迭代的工具连接前沿智能体的编程与决策能力，同时保留每次动作执行后的模型级决策，表明这种分工有望提升机器人操作的效率与成功率。

Abstract: Frontier models can control robots, but reasoning through every reach, grasp, and retreat makes manipulation slow and token-intensive. We revisit code as policy with a different division of labor: models build executable tools, code handles multi-phase motions, and models decide what to do next. We introduce URAI (Universal Robot-Agent Interface), which couples a programming agent that constructs robot tools with an execution agent that uses them in a feedback loop. The programming agent writes reusable and task-specific tools from task intent and refines them through execution feedback and human guidance. The execution agent selects and parameterizes these tools from current observations; each call runs a complete motion locally before returning control to the agent. Unlike delegating subsequent decisions to a generated program, this design retains model-level decision-making between tool executions. Validated tool revisions persist across episodes without updating foundation-model weights, and a shared GUI and API make the same tools available to humans and agents. Across five RoboDojo tasks and four frozen execution agents, URAI raises aggregate success from 18.0% to 53.0% relative to direct fingertip control, with the largest gain on Swap Blocks; with the same tools, a program written in advance reaches only 24% against 56% for two agents deciding after each call. Three of the four agents also finish episodes 1.3-1.5 times faster with 1.5-1.7 times fewer execution-agent output tokens; DeepSeek-V4-Flash's cost barely changes. We further evaluate URAI on seven real-world AgileX dual-arm tasks, spanning object manipulation, cloth folding, and human-interactive tic-tac-toe. URAI connects the coding and decision-making capabilities of frontier agents, organizing robot control around reusable tools that agents can both invoke and revise.

</details>


### [100] [Sparse-WAM: Accelerating World Action Models via Action-Guided Sparse Imagination](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38984)
*Xinling Xie,Haodong Wang,Jiazhi Mi,Zhiming Liu,Zicong Hong,Xiaoyi Pang,Qianli Liu,Yangjia Hu,Ying Chen,Zhengyang Yan,Song Guo*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Sparse-WAM通过动作引导的未来帧令牌稀疏化及高效执行引擎，加速世界动作模型推理，同时基本保持任务表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 世界动作模型联合预测未来视觉状态与动作，但去噪过程中反复处理密集的未来帧令牌，推理开销较高。既有剪枝方法侧重视觉保真，未充分考虑令牌对动作预测的相关性。

Method: 提出免训练框架Sparse-WAM，利用连续去噪步骤间动作令牌到未来帧令牌的注意力空间分布具有较强重叠这一观察，选择与动作相关的帧内区域并保留跨帧上下文。其Pilot引擎通过轻量评分和跨步骤复用令牌选择，降低评分与令牌打包开销。

Result: 在LIBERO（FastWAM-Joint）和RoboLab-120（Cosmos 3 Edge）上，相较于NVIDIA RTX 4090上的稠密即时推理，推理速度分别提升约2.0倍和1.8倍，同时基本保持任务表现。

Conclusion: 该方法表明，依据动作相关性选择未来视觉令牌，并控制稀疏化自身的执行开销，可有效提升世界动作模型的推理效率。

Abstract: World-action models (WAMs) leverage pretrained video models to improve generalization in robot control by jointly predicting future visual states and actions. This capability comes at a substantial inference cost, as dense future-frame tokens are repeatedly processed during denoising. Prior methods address this by token pruning that prioritizes visual fidelity to reduce denoising costs in video diffusion models. However, these methods do not use action relevance to determine which future-frame tokens to retain during joint denoising in WAMs. In this paper, we propose Sparse-WAM, a training-free framework for action-guided sparse imagination that selectively processes future-frame tokens to accelerate WAM inference. We observe substantial overlap in the spatial distribution of attention from action tokens to future-frame tokens (action-to-future attention) between consecutive denoising steps, despite continued updates to the future representations. Motivated by this, we develop Action-Guided Token Selection to retain frame-specific action-relevant regions together with cross-frame context. However, a naive implementation can incur attention-scoring and token-packing overhead that offsets the computational savings from pruning. We therefore introduce Pilot, an efficient engine that reduces sparse inference overhead through lightweight scoring and cross-step reuse of token selections. On LIBERO with FastWAM-Joint and RoboLab-120 with Cosmos 3 Edge, Sparse-WAM achieves inference speedups of approximately $2.0\times$ and $1.8\times$, respectively, over dense eager inference on an NVIDIA RTX 4090, while largely preserving task performance.

</details>


### [101] [DrivingBench: Can Vision-Language Models Drive a Toyota Corolla?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38948)
*Aditya Ramabadran,Simon Mahns,Tobias Gessler*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: DrivingBench评测通用视觉语言模型驾驶真实汽车的能力；测试中仅GPT-6 Astra完成了规定路线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数字基准表现出色，并不意味着模型具备现实世界中的感知与行动能力。研究旨在检验模型能否在真实车辆、持续运动和推理延迟等条件下完成长时程驾驶任务。

Method: 研究者让模型通过三种工具接收丰田卡罗拉的摄像头画面，并直接控制转向与车速，在停车场锥桶路线中低速行驶。评测GPT-6 Astra、Claude Fable 5.1、GPT-5.6 Sol和Grok 4.6，每个模型在原生工具环境中最多尝试三次，并考察保留上下文后的表现变化。

Result: 四个模型中，只有GPT-6 Astra在第二次尝试时完成路线；其余尝试均未达到路线的一半。四个模型中有两个在后续尝试中取得明显进步。工具输出格式与任务描述方式也会影响模型是否实际驾驶或选择拒绝。

Conclusion: DrivingBench将模型评测从数字环境扩展到真实车辆操作，揭示了延迟、连续交互和任务表述对实际驾驶表现的重要影响。作者还发布了测试工具、提示词、路线图及包含视频和遥测数据的运行记录，以支持复现。

Abstract: Frontier models excel at many digital benchmarks, yet their ability to drive a real car, an everyday human skill, remains largely untested. We present DrivingBench, to our knowledge the first benchmark where general-purpose vision-language models must drive a real car. Through three tools, the models see camera frames from a Toyota Corolla and directly command its steering and velocity around a parking lot cone course at low speeds. The car may continue moving while the model thinks and new commands replace the currently running one, so inference latency is part of the task, testing the models' abilities to observe, act, monitor, recover, and complete a long-horizon objective under such constraints. We benchmark GPT-6 Astra, Claude Fable 5.1, GPT-5.6 Sol, and Grok 4.6 in vendor-native harnesses (Codex, Claude Code, Cursor) with up to three attempts each in one conversation; Astra is the only model to finish the course, on its second attempt, with no other attempt passing 50% of the course. Two of the four models improved materially across attempts with retained context. We also detail the design principles behind our action interface, and show how the tool output format and the framing of the task combined to determine whether models would drive at all or refuse. We release our harness, prompts, course map, and traces with video and telemetry for reproducibility.

</details>


### [102] [Efficient Multi-Modal Planning with Reward-Guided Preference Optimization for Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38862)
*Chenglin Chen,Lujia Wang,Xinhu Zheng,Jun Ma,Haoang Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: EMPlan通过稀疏锚点与偏移细化实现高效多模态轨迹规划，并以奖励引导微调提升安全性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有端到端规划方法在计算效率与安全保障方面存在不足：模仿学习易受因果混淆影响，规则评分计算开销大且目标可能错位，偏好学习则依赖严格的成对标注，限制数据利用。

Method: 构建由稀疏轨迹锚点和偏移细化模块组成的混合架构：先以低延迟生成粗略候选轨迹，再进行精细调整。训练采用预训练与奖励引导微调两阶段方案，微调时结合规则奖励信号和非配对偏好监督，引导策略选择更安全的轨迹。

Result: 在非反应式 NAVSIM 基准上的评估显示，EMPlan兼顾规划精度与计算效率，并在实时约束下表现出较强性能。

Conclusion: EMPlan将高效的多模态轨迹生成与无需增加推理开销的安全导向训练相结合，为实时自动驾驶规划提供了一种兼顾效率与安全性的方案。

Abstract: Safe and efficient trajectory planning is essential in autonomous driving. However, existing end-to-end approaches often fall short in both computational efficiency and safety guarantees. Methods based on imitation learning suffer from causal confusion, while rule-based scoring approaches often incur heavy computational overhead and suffer from objective misalignment. Additionally, preference-based methods rely on strict pairwise annotations, limiting data utilization. To overcome these limitations, we propose EMPlan, an efficient multi-modal trajectory planning method powered by reward-guided fine-tuning. We design a hybrid architecture that combines sparse anchors with an offset refinement module for efficient multi-modal trajectory prediction. Sparse anchors provide coarse trajectory candidates with low latency, which are subsequently refined by the offset module for higher prediction accuracy. To enhance safety without incurring additional inference costs, we adopt a two-stage training paradigm consisting of pretraining and reward-guided fine-tuning. During fine-tuning, we leverage rule-based reward signals and unpaired preference supervision to refine the pretrained policy toward safer trajectory selection. We evaluate EMPlan on the non-reactive NAVSIM benchmark, where it strikes a favorable balance between planning accuracy and efficiency, demonstrating superior performance under real-time constraints.

</details>


### [103] [Online Evolution Strategy for Flow-Matching VLA Policies via Self-Supervised Trajectory Distribution Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38855)
*Gongxin Yao,Yongsheng Zhao,Jiayin Deng,Deng Liang,Han Gao,Lei Zhao,Baoping Cheng*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Online-ES 利用交互反馈在线优化 Flow Matching 视觉—语言—动作模型生成的动作轨迹分布，无需价值模型或优势计算。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Flow Matching 模型能够生成多样化动作，但其轨迹分布可能同时包含成功与失败行为，且较多概率质量落在不利区域，因此需要在线调整策略。

Method: 在动作轨迹空间中对采样轨迹进行扰动，并依据执行结果构造自监督均方误差目标，将进化搜索方向传递至模型参数空间。论文证明该目标可无偏估计最优进化方向，并利用失败经验作为负反馈，引导策略避开已探索的失败区域。

Result: 仿真和真实环境实验表明，Online-ES 能有效提升策略表现，效果可与强化学习微调相当，且不需要学习价值模型或计算优势。

Conclusion: 该方法通过直接在轨迹空间开展进化探索，将交互反馈用于在线优化生成式策略，为 Flow Matching 视觉—语言—动作模型提供了一种无需价值估计的适应方案。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models based on generative frameworks, such as Flow Matching, have recently achieved impressive performance in robotic manipulation. Unlike deterministic policies, Flow Matching enables VLA models to learn conditional action trajectory distributions, where latent noise vectors induce different actions under the same task scenario. However, we observe that these distributions are often ill-formed, with successful and failed behaviors coexisting while considerable probability mass remains in unfavorable regions. To this end, we propose Online-ES, an online adaptation framework for Flow Matching VLAs based on Evolution Strategy (ES), which refines the learned action trajectory distribution through interaction feedback. Instead of pruning the latent noise space, our method performs evolutionary exploration directly in the action trajectory space, where diverse trajectories generated by Flow Matching provide candidate solutions for adaptation. By perturbing sampled trajectories and evaluating their execution outcomes, we derive a self-supervised MSE objective that transfers the evolution direction from trajectory space into model parameter space. Mathematically, we prove that the proposed objective provides an unbiased estimator of the optimal evolution direction. Moreover, we also incorporate failure experiences as negative feedback to regularize the evolution direction, steering the policy away from previously explored failure regions. Experiments in both simulation and real-world environments demonstrate that Online-ES achieves policy improvement comparable to reinforcement fine-tuning, without learning a value model or computing advantages.

</details>


### [104] [SynIL: Leveraging Synergy for Offline Imitation Learning from Imperfect Demonstration Datasets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38225)
*Yuto Tanaka,Kyo Kutsuzawa,Martina Doku,Dai Owaki,Mitsuhiro Hayashibe*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出SynIL，一种无需人工标注或专家参考数据的示范质量评估框架，通过量化运动协同结构生成密集奖励，提升离线模仿学习在含噪数据上的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模示范数据常包含次优或噪声轨迹，导致模仿学习性能显著下降。现有方法依赖人工筛选专家数据或任务特定启发式规则，难以扩展，因此需要自动化、无标签且通用的示范质量评估方法。

Method: SynIL基于运动协同理论，将动作中的低维协调结构作为运动熟练度指标，算法化地量化协同结构的表现，并通过自监督奖励回归，将其转化为转移级密集奖励信号，用于离线强化学习或示范数据加权。

Result: 在D4RL运动控制基准和多人体Robomimic操作数据集上的实验表明，基于运动协同生成的奖励与真实奖励具有较强相关性。SynIL显著优于行为克隆，在稀疏奖励的人类遥操作场景中，其性能达到或超过使用真实环境奖励训练的离线强化学习方法。

Conclusion: SynIL证明运动协同可作为跨任务的示范质量代理指标，为含噪离线示范数据提供了一种可扩展的无标签奖励构造方案，并能有效提升模仿学习和离线强化学习性能。

Abstract: Imitation learning enables robots to acquire complex skills directly from massive demonstration datasets, but its performance degrades severely when datasets are contaminated with suboptimal or noisy demonstrations. While prior quality-assessment methods attempt to filter or reweight data, they typically rely on manual pre-selection of expert reference data or task-specific heuristics, limiting scalability. To address this challenge, we introduce SynIL (Synergy-based Imitation Learning), a novel framework for automated, label-free demonstration quality assessment in offline reinforcement learning. Grounded in neuroscientific evidence that motor synergy, a low-dimensional coordinated structure in movement, correlates directly with motor proficiency, SynIL algorithmically quantifies synergy manifestation to generate dense, transition-level reward signals via self-supervised reward regression. Comprehensive evaluations on D4RL locomotion benchmarks and multi-human Robomimic manipulation datasets demonstrate that synergy-derived rewards correlate strongly with ground-truth rewards. Furthermore, SynIL substantially outperforms Behavior Cloning (BC) and achieves performance comparable to, and in sparse-reward human teleoperation scenarios, superior to, offline reinforcement learning trained on true environment rewards.

</details>


### [105] [Multi-Link Safety Filtering for VLA Policies Around Moving Hazards](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.40007)
*Yatharth Agarwal,Vijay Raghunathan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种无需重新训练的运行时安全屏蔽器，使预训练视觉—语言—动作策略在杂乱环境中操作时减少与移动障碍物的碰撞。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 仅完成任务并不能说明策略安全：机械臂可能碰撞无关物体。现有保护还需覆盖末端执行器以外的臂部、跟踪移动障碍物，并适应与策略共享的机载计算资源。

Method: 用五个椭球近似夹爪、腕部和前臂，并通过单一屏障优化程序过滤每条运动指令。利用重置时的 RGB-D 感知拟合障碍物禁入椭球，再以稀疏光流跟踪其中心，无需重复检测或拟合。另通过缩短视觉—语言前缀、减少流匹配步数降低策略推理时延。

Result: 六种仿真障碍物运动条件下，碰撞率从 65.62% 降至 27.27%，无碰撞任务完成率从 29.35% 升至 50.43%。消融实验显示，覆盖机械臂连杆优于仅保护末端执行器，光流跟踪可弥补障碍物位置冻结造成的大部分保护损失。屏障程序 CPU 端 99 分位时延为 2.2 毫秒；优化后，集成 GPU 上的策略调用时延从 343 毫秒降至 177.3 毫秒。实体机械臂实验中，屏蔽组 16 次操作有 3 次触碰障碍物，未屏蔽组为 11 次。

Conclusion: 该方法在不重新训练策略的前提下，兼顾移动障碍物防护、机械臂多部位覆盖与边缘设备实时运行，并在仿真和实体实验中减少碰撞；但实体实验规模有限，仍需更广泛验证。

Abstract: A vision-language-action (VLA) policy can finish a manipulation task while knocking over objects unrelated to it, so task success alone does not show that the policy is safe to deploy in clutter. We study how to keep a pretrained VLA policy clear of such hazards at run time without retraining it, which requires guarding more of the arm than the end effector, following the hazard as it moves, and sharing onboard compute with the policy. Our training-free shield covers the gripper, wrist, and forearm with five ellipsoids and filters every commanded motion through one barrier program against a keep-out ellipsoid fitted from RGB-D perception at reset. Sparse optical flow then carries that ellipsoid's center along with the hazard, with no repeated detection or refitting. Over six simulated hazard-motion conditions, the shield lowers collision from $65.62\%$ to $27.27\%$ and raises safe-success, task completion without collision, from $29.35\%$ to $50.43\%$. Ablations show that guarding the arm links protects beyond end-effector shielding, and that tracking recovers most of the protection lost when the hazard estimate is frozen at reset. On heterogeneous edge hardware, the five-ellipsoid barrier runs on the CPU in $2.2$~ms at the 99th percentile, and trimming the vision--language prefix and taking fewer flow-matching steps shortens each $\pi_{0.5}$ policy call on the integrated GPU from $343$ to $177.3$~ms. On a physical SO-101 arm across four tasks, the arm touched the hazard in 3 of 16 shielded episodes versus 11 of 16 unshielded ones. Project page: this https URL

</details>


### [106] [UniWAM Technical Report: Unified Mobile Manipulation via Mixed-Stream World-Action Modeling and Manipulation Anchor Pose Supervision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39388)
*Wei Xue,Keliang Liu,Mingzhang Cui,Jinhua Xie,Jinjie Wei,Jianan Hou,Jingcheng Lu,Lintao Wang,Kaixiang Qiu,Yizhou Liu,Xinghai Ye,Jinghang Han,Mingcheng Li,Jie Gu,Shunli Wang,Lihua Zhang,Dingkang Yang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: UniWAM 将移动导航与物体操作整合到共享模型中，并利用大规模 MAP-Data 提升导航至可操作位姿的能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 移动操作中的导航与操作在动作空间和视觉需求上差异显著，增加了统一策略学习的难度；真实场景中带有明确操作就绪位姿标注的导航数据也难以低成本、大规模采集。

Method: 提出 UniWAM，以共享骨干网络结合导航和操作专用动作编码器及输出头，支持两类数据联合训练、独立推理或并行推理。提出操作锚点位姿（MAP）监督，并通过自动化流程从大规模三维场景构建包含超过150万条轨迹、7500小时数据的 MAP-Data；数据提供目标框、图像平面 MAP 坐标及末端执行器投影轨迹等监督信号。

Result: 在 MAP-Bench 上，UniWAM 相较最强外部基线的位置误差降低30.1%，朝向误差降低44.0%。在24项真实机器人任务中，模型在 MAP 导航和移动操作方面取得领先表现，操作能力也具有竞争力。

Conclusion: 大规模 MAP 位姿监督与导航、操作分流建模相结合，可有效提升移动操作中的导航定位表现，并支持统一模型处理两类任务。

Abstract: Mobile manipulation requires precise navigation to a manipulation-ready pose followed by reliable object interaction. These two stages differ in action spaces and visual requirements, which complicates unified policy learning. In addition, collecting diverse real-world navigation data with explicit manipulation-ready pose supervision remains costly and difficult to scale. We introduce UniWAM, a unified mixed-stream world-action model with separate action encoders and output heads for navigation and manipulation, sharing a common backbone. This design supports joint representation learning on independently sampled navigation and manipulation data. UniWAM supports independent inference for either stream and batch-parallel inference for both. We further introduce Manipulation Anchor Pose (MAP) supervision for where to stop and how to orient for manipulation. An automated pipeline constructs MAP-Data from large-scale 3D scenes, yielding over 1.5 million episodes and 7,500 hours. MAP-Data provides per-frame target-object bounding boxes and image-plane MAP coordinates as auxiliary navigation supervision. Together with projected end-effector trajectories for manipulation, these prediction targets provide stream-specific image-plane supervision for action learning from egocentric observations. With large-scale MAP-Data, UniWAM outperforms the strongest external baselines on our MAP-Bench by 30.1\% in position error and 44.0\% in heading error. Across 24 real-robot tasks, UniWAM achieves leading results in MAP navigation and mobile manipulation, with competitive manipulation performance. We have released code, data, and benchmark.

</details>


### [107] [Beyond the Current Scene: Event-Referential Grasping with Active View Selection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39375)
*Hyunjoon Lee,Haebeom Jung,Eunsung Cha,Daeun Lee,Yu-Chiang Frank Wang,Jaesung Choe,Jaesik Park*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: BeyondSCe 是一种零样本机器人抓取系统，可依据历史事件识别并抓取当前可见或被遮挡的目标物体及其部件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人们可能通过物体在过去事件中的作用来指代目标，而非使用名称或外观；请求到来时，目标还可能已被遮挡。系统需要结合事件历史与当前场景完成目标定位和抓取。

Method: 系统使用预训练模型，无需额外任务专用训练。对于遮挡目标，它将从事件历史中提取的先验信息与当前场景几何结合，选择有望揭示目标的相机视角。实验使用单个腕载 RGB-D 相机。

Result: 实机实验中，目标初始可见和被遮挡时的抓取成功率分别为 76% 和 77%，高于各条件下最强基线的 40% 和 55%。在另外四个严重遮挡场景中，相较于已知目标真实 3D 边界框的主动感知基线，成功率从 75% 提升至 95%，平均观测视角数从 3.35 降至 2.20。

Conclusion: 结果表明，结合事件记忆与主动视角选择，能够提升机器人对事件指代目标的零样本抓取能力，尤其适用于目标被遮挡的场景。

Abstract: A robot that observes people interacting with objects should be able to carry out later requests that refer back to those interactions. Such requests may specify a grasp target by the role it played in a past event rather than by its name or appearance. Moreover, the target may no longer be visible when the robot is asked to act. We present BeyondSCe, a zero-shot robotic grasping system for this event-referential setting. Given the event history and the current scene, the system identifies the requested object or part and localizes it for grasping. If the target is occluded, it combines an event prior recovered from the history with current scene geometry to select camera viewpoints likely to reveal the target. The system uses pretrained models without additional task-specific training.
