In real-robot experiments with a single wrist-mounted RGB-D camera, it achieves grasp success rates of 76% and 77% for initially visible and occluded targets, respectively, compared with 40% and 55% for the strongest baseline in each condition. On four additional scenes with heavy occlusion, it increases grasp success rates from 75% to 95% while reducing the mean number of views from 3.35 to 2.20, compared with an active-perception baseline given the target's ground-truth 3D bounding box.

</details>


### [108] [The Planning Limits of Latent World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39235)
*Ali Alrasheed,Basim Azam,Naveed Akhtar*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 研究指出，世界模型的规划能力主要受想象轨迹长度限制；在可规划范围内，它能提升机器人任务表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 既有研究多展示世界模型能做什么，却较少分析其预测何时有助于规划、何时失效。

Method: 基于五种冻结的自监督视觉骨干网络构建动作条件预测器，在 Meta-World 操作任务和 BridgeData V2 真实机器人交互中评估，并比较不同预测器规模、训练轨迹长度、编码器及规划策略。

Result: 五步预测器通常只能可靠地为未来五至十个控制步的目标排序，而任务目标位于十六至五十三步之外；扩大预测器或延长训练轨迹未能扩大这一范围。即使使用真实模拟器的完美预测，目标从五步移至二十步时，成功率也从92%降至41%。远距离目标下，纯想象成功率为23%，加入反馈的MPC为30%，想象至目标距离为47%，采用邻近专家子目标为76%。在可规划范围内，用世界模型从VLA提出的八个动作中进行选择，可将成功率从65%提升至77%。

Conclusion: 世界模型的有效规划范围受想象轨迹覆盖距离制约，单纯提升预测规模或训练长度不足以解决远距离规划问题。可通过延长想象轨迹、设置近距离子目标或结合反馈规划来改善表现；在其有效范围内，世界模型也能增强VLA策略。

Abstract: World models offer a promising way to help robots understand how the physical world evolves and plan complex behaviours through imagination. Yet existing studies mainly demonstrate what these models can accomplish, leaving unclear when their predictions remain useful for planning and where they fail. We study this question using action-conditioned predictors built on five frozen self-supervised visual backbones: V-JEPA 2, V-JEPA 2.1, VideoMAEv2, VideoPrism, and DINOv2. We use frozen backbones to test representations intended to transfer across environments. We evaluate these models on diverse Meta-World manipulation tasks and real-robot interactions from BridgeData V2. We find that a world model guides action selection reliably only when the goal lies within, or slightly beyond, the trajectory it imagines during planning. With five-step rollouts, the length the predictor was trained on, the world model ranks actions reliably only for targets five to ten control steps ahead, whereas task goals lie 16 to 53 steps away. Neither an 81-fold larger predictor nor longer-rollout training extends this range; the encoder affects both range and closed-loop success, with V-JEPA 2.1 performing most consistently. More fundamentally, the limit persists under perfect prediction: using the real simulator, success falls from 92% to 41% as the target moves from five to twenty steps ahead of a five-step rollout. Planning therefore requires either longer imagined trajectories or closer subgoals. For distant goals, pure imagination succeeds in 23% of episodes, planning with feedback (MPC) raises success to 30%, imagining as far as the goal to 47%, and nearby expert subgoals to 76%. Used within its plannable range, a world model can also improve a vision-language-action (VLA) policy: choosing among eight actions the VLA proposes raises its success from 65% to 77% across 16 different tasks.

</details>


### [109] [Exploiting Vulnerabilities: Universal Adversarial Attacks on Vision-Language-Action Models in Robotics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39178)
*Songhua Yang,Ziyu Liu,Yuanwei Liu,Xuetao Li,Xuanye Fei,He Huang,Zheng Wang,Miao Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 论文提出一种通用对抗物体：通过优化球体表面纹理，在机器人视野中干扰视觉—语言—动作（VLA）模型的操作表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA模型直接与物理环境及人交互，微小安全漏洞可能导致严重失误，因此需要评估其面对视觉对抗干扰时的脆弱性。

Method: 设计多层级攻击框架，联合干扰轨迹规划、任务执行与动作控制；将具有优化纹理的球体放入机器人视野，并在仿真和真实机器人环境中测试。

Result: 针对Pi0和RDT两种VLA模型，该方法使平均任务成功率下降31.2%至39.9%；在复杂场景中，成功率接近于零。

Conclusion: 实验表明，单一通用对抗物体可显著削弱多种VLA模型的操作可靠性，凸显了机器人安全评估与防御的必要性。

Abstract: Recently, Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models have revolutionized robotic manipulation by seamlessly integrating visual perception, language understanding, and action generation in an end-to-end learning framework. However, since these models are designed to interact directly with the physical world and humans, their security is critical, and even small vulnerabilities can lead to catastrophic failures. In this work, we propose the Universal Adversarial Object, a sphere with optimized surface texture that significantly degrades task success rates when placed within the robot's field of view. Specifically, our approach introduces a multi-level attack framework that jointly disrupts trajectory planning, task execution, and action control. We validate our method in both simulated and real-world robotic settings. Experimental results demonstrate that the adversarial object reduces the average task success rates by 31.2%-39.9% for two representative VLA models (Pi0 and RDT), with success rates dropping to near zero in complex scenarios. Index Terms--Vision-Language-Action models, adversarial attack, robotic security, universal adversarial object

</details>


### [110] [DiFF: Doppler-informed Flow Matching for Human Motion Flow](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39098)
*Kai Wang,Mingle Zhao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: DiFF 利用多普勒速度线索与基于 KAN 的条件流匹配，估计稀疏、含噪 4D 毫米波雷达点云中的非刚性场景流。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 4D 毫米波雷达点云稀疏且噪声较大，使非刚性运动流估计成为病态问题。现有方法偏重刚体运动，且未充分利用雷达自带的多普勒速度信息。

Method: 提出生成式框架 DiFF，将多普勒信息引导的运动先验融入基于 Kolmogorov-Arnold Network（KAN）的条件流匹配模型；通过 KAN 注意力机制提取特征，并以先验引导的生成过程约束解空间。

Result: 实验显示，DiFF 在多个真实数据集上取得领先表现；在 mmBody 基准上，3D 端点误差降至毫米级。

Conclusion: 将多普勒速度线索作为运动先验，有助于缓解稀疏雷达点云中非刚性场景流估计的病态性；DiFF 展现了用于隐私保护型人机交互运动感知的潜力。

Abstract: Perceiving human motion via privacy-preserving 4D millimeter-wave (mmWave) radar is critical for next-generation human-robot interaction (HRI), where point cloud scene flow serves as a foundational motion representation. Yet the extreme sparsity and noise of 4D radar point clouds make non-rigid motion flow estimation severely ill-posed--a challenge that existing rigid-centric methods and prior works fail to adequately address, largely because they neglect the rich Doppler velocity cues inherent in 4D radar. We propose DiFF, a generative framework that marries Doppler-informed motion priors with a Kolmogorov-Arnold Network (KAN)-based conditional flow matching model. At its core, a KAN-attention mechanism enables expressive feature extraction, while a prior-guided generative process harnesses Doppler cues to regularize the ill-posed solution space. Extensive experiments show that DiFF achieves state-of-the-art (SOTA) performance across diverse real-world datasets, reducing 3D endpoint error to the millimeter scale on the mmBody benchmark.

</details>


### [111] [What to Attend, What to Keep: Skill-Conditioned Visuotactile Representation with Progress-Guided Event Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38494)
*Amir-Hossein Shahidzadeh,Seungjae Lee,Eadom Dessalene,Shanthosh Raaj Mohanram Mageswari,Soroush Etemad,Furong Huang,Cornelia Fermüller,Yiannis Aloimonos*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 论文提出按操作技能动态融合视觉与触觉，并利用稀疏事件记忆改善接触丰富任务中的技能进度估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 不同操作阶段对视觉和触觉的依赖会变化，但现有多模态策略通常采用固定时间上下文与融合方式，难以适应各技能的感知需求。

Method: 提出技能条件化表示：由当前待执行技能控制各模态短期观测令牌的融合，并关注由最近 K 个已执行技能的终止观测构成的稀疏事件记忆。

Result: 在三项接触丰富任务的技能进度估计中，相较微调后的先进进度模型，滑移检测延迟降低 87%；相较纯视觉消融，扭转完成判断延迟降低 67.5%；稀疏事件记忆使盲搜任务的进度误差降低 92%。

Conclusion: 性能提升主要出现在完成状态取决于接触信息或任务历史的情形，说明多模态学习不仅需要改进策略架构，也应重视观测的构建方式。

Abstract: Robotic manipulation integrates vision, touch, and language, whose importance shifts across stages: vision guides reaching, while touch, through its evolution over time, decides grasping, alignment, and contact. Yet existing multi-modal manipulation policies typically use fixed temporal contexts and fusion strategies, despite shifts in what each modality contributes across different skills. We study how vision and touch should be combined at the level of primitive skills, asking what each skill needs from each sensor, and propose a skill-conditioned representation in which the queried skill conditions fusion over modality-specific short-term observation tokens while attending to a sparse event memory that retains terminal observations from the last $K$ executed skills. Evaluated by skill progress estimation on three contact-rich tasks, it reduces slip-detection delay by 87% against fine-tuned SOTA progress models, twist-completion delay by 67.5% against a vision-only ablation, and progress error on a blind search task by 92% through sparse event memory. Gains concentrate exactly where completion is defined by contact or task history. More broadly, our results suggest that observation formation not only policy architecture is a central challenge in multi-modal representation. Project Website: this http URL

</details>


### [112] [TrafficSignBench: Rule-Centric Closed-Loop Evaluation of Traffic-Sign Compliance in Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38463)
*Victoria Smirnova,Viktoriia Zinkovich,Gregorii Bukhtuev,Artem Belyaev,Andrey Kuznetsov,Denis Shepelev,Vlad Shakhuro*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出 TrafficSignBench，用于系统评估自动驾驶规划器对交通标志规则的遵守情况，并展示其与常规驾驶指标之间可能存在的落差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 驾驶得分、到达率和碰撞率等汇总指标无法直接衡量交通规则合规性，因此规划器即使基准成绩较高，也可能出现不安全或违法行为。

Method: 结合真实地图仿真与面向规则的程序化场景生成，覆盖34种交通标志对应的规则；为每条规则实现自动检测器，并构建29,000个道路场景、29类测试场景及5,800个测试场景。此外，通过加入明确的交通标志约束，将现有规划器转化为遵规轨迹专家，以生成用于微调的示范轨迹。

Result: 评估表明，当前自动驾驶规划器即使在常规指标上表现较强，仍可能存在较差的交通规则合规性。

Conclusion: TrafficSignBench提供了可扩展、可解释且针对具体规则的评估框架；利用显式规则约束生成专家轨迹，也为提升规划器合规性提供了数据途径。

Abstract: Autonomous driving planners are typically evaluated using aggregate metrics such as driving score, destination rate, and collision rate, which do not explicitly measure compliance with traffic rules. As a result, planners can achieve high benchmark scores while still exhibiting unsafe or illegal behaviors, limiting their applicability to real-world deployment. To address this gap, we introduce TrafficSignBench, a large-scale, traffic sign-centric benchmark for systematic and interpretable evaluation of traffic-rule compliance in autonomous driving. Our framework combines real-map-based simulation for realistic road layouts with rule-targeted procedural scenario generation for scalable and balanced coverage of underrepresented rules. We implement traffic rules corresponding to 34 traffic signs, each equipped with an automatic rule checker for detecting violations during closed-loop execution. This design yields 29,000 diverse road scenes and 29 distinct testing scenario types, enabling controlled evaluation of rule-specific planner behavior. We construct 5,800 testing scenes and demonstrate that current autonomous driving planners can exhibit poor traffic-rule compliance despite strong performance on standard evaluation metrics. To address this limitation, we transform existing planners into rule-compliant trajectory experts via explicit traffic-sign constraints, enabling scalable generation of high-quality oracle trajectories for fine-tuning.

</details>


### [113] [BIND: Binding 3D Robot Actions to 2D Image Features](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38443)
*Cameron Smith,Arsh Tangri,Vitor Guizilini,Yue Wang,Zubair Irshad,Sergey Zakharov*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: BIND 将候选三维机器人动作与其在图像中的视觉特征显式绑定，提升了机器人策略的数据效率及对视角和物体位置变化的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有策略通常从单一全局视觉特征回归动作，必须仅凭示范学习动作与局部图像特征的对应关系，因此对相机视角和物体位置的细微变化较脆弱，且数据效率不足。

Method: 离散化末端执行器的候选位置，通过相机几何将每个候选位置投影到各相机视图，并关联对应的预训练图像特征；策略对候选位置及其绑定特征组合进行评分，以选择动作。

Result: 真实机器人实验显示，BIND 在仅有 5 条示范时即可在测试任务中取得近乎完美的成功率；面对未见物体位置和大幅相机视角变化时仍能稳健执行，而坐标回归基线则完全失效。研究还评估了长时程任务执行和灵巧操作能力。

Conclusion: 通过相机几何显式提供三维动作与二维视觉特征之间的对应关系，BIND 能显著改善数据效率和分布外鲁棒性。

Abstract: We introduce BIND, a new action representation for visuomotor robot policies that binds 3D robot actions to their corresponding 2D image features, yielding strong data efficiency gains and robustness to out-of-distribution object positions and camera viewpoints. The action heads of current robot policies are typically formulated as an MLP regression from a single global feature vector produced by a pre-trained vision encoder. This global formulation requires the policy network to discover, from demonstrations alone, the relationship between target robot actions and the image features they project onto. The consequence is that although modern image features are semantically descriptive, spatially robust, and even multiview-consistent, the policies built on them are brittle to subtle changes in camera viewpoint and object placement--and surprisingly data-inefficient. BIND closes this gap by supplying the action-feature relationship through camera geometry rather than learning: it discretizes a volume of candidate end effector positions, attaches each candidate to the pre-trained features at its projection in each camera view, and selects actions by scoring each candidate's position and image-bound feature combination. On a real robot, we study data efficiency and out-of-distribution robustness to unseen object positions and camera viewpoints, as well as general long-horizon task execution and dexterity. We find BIND to be highly data-efficient and robust: it achieves near-perfect success on tasks with as few as 5 demonstrations, and degrades gracefully under steep camera-viewpoint shifts and held-out object positions where coordinate-regression baselines completely fail.

</details>


### [114] [DynaHarness: A Dynamic Physical Harness for Self-Evolving Robot Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.40306)
*Haoyuan Deng,Jiebin Liu,Tengxiao Zhang,Langning Yan,Hongye Cao,Ziwei Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: DynaHarness 将语义规划与物理执行纳入共享执行契约，并利用失败证据定向改进能力；在 LIBERO-Pro 上显著优于冻结策略和名义式重规划。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预训练机器人策略虽能提供动作先验，但长时程操作需要协调较慢的语义推理与较快的物理交互。仅凭回合级失败难以判断应修改系统的哪个组件。

Method: 系统由慢速推理模块提出能力与符号论证，快速执行模块负责指令落地、监控、拒绝未解决动作、替换能力及请求重规划。执行契约限制每条已接受指令，并记录分析技能、恢复技能和冻结视觉—语言—动作策略的执行证据。系统据此定位故障、定向修订可复用能力或执行机制，并通过成对回归检查决定是否纳入修订。

Result: 在 LIBERO-Pro 的 800 个新采样初始状态上，DynaHarness 达到 75.2%，冻结策略为 17.5%。使用相同能力库时，动态执行达到 74.0%，高于名义式单步重规划的 63.9%。

Conclusion: 结果表明，动态执行治理能够有效落地、监控并协调现有能力，同时借助失败归因和回归验证形成能力修订闭环，提升长时程操作表现。

Abstract: Pretrained robot policies provide useful action priors, but long-horizon manipulation still requires coordination between semantic reasoning and physical execution. Semantic reasoning operates at a coarser timescale than physical interaction, while episode-level failures provide limited guidance on which system component should be revised. We propose DynaHarness, a dynamic physical harness that couples semantic reasoning with physical governance through a shared execution contract and turns failure evidence into validated capability revisions. To be more specific, the slow brain proposes capabilities and symbolic arguments, while the fast brain grounds and monitors commands, refuses unresolved actions, substitutes capabilities, and requests replans when needed. The physical execution contract bounds each accepted command and records execution evidence across analytic skills, recovery skills, and the frozen VLA. Failure attribution localizes faults in these records and directs targeted revisions of reusable capabilities or execution mechanisms. Paired regression checks govern admission or rejection, closing the self-evolution loop. On LIBERO-Pro, DynaHarness achieves 75.2% on 800 newly sampled initial states, compared with 17.5% for the frozen policy. With the same capability library, full dynamic execution reaches 74.0% versus 63.9% under nominal one-step replanning. This demonstrates the value of DynaHarness as a dynamic physical harness that governs how existing capabilities are grounded, monitored, and coordinated during execution. Our project page is at this https URL .

</details>


### [115] [STARS: From Spatiotemporal Dynamics to Social Representations in Human-Robot Interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.40245)
*Nathan Tsoi,Michael J. Munje,Tejas Oberoi,Rishab Maheshwari,Pengen Zheng,Tanush Chauhan,Peter Stone,Joydeep Biswas*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出 SocialNav-SUB，用于评估视觉语言模型在真实社会机器人导航场景中的理解能力；实验显示，当前最佳模型仍不及规则方法和人类共识基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 社会机器人导航要求系统理解复杂场景中的空间与时间关系及人的意图。尽管视觉语言模型具备识别和推理能力，但其是否能满足这些需求，尚缺乏系统评估。

Method: 构建 SocialNav-SUB，这是面向真实社会机器人导航场景的视觉问答数据集与基准，涵盖空间、时空和社会推理任务，并将视觉语言模型与人类及基于规则的方法进行比较。

Result: 表现最佳的视觉语言模型在答案上与人类有一定一致性，但整体仍逊于较简单的规则方法和人类共识基线，显示其社会场景理解存在明显不足。

Conclusion: SocialNav-SUB 为评估和改进视觉语言模型在社会机器人导航中的应用提供了统一框架，也凸显了当前模型走向真实场景应用所面临的理解能力差距。

Abstract: Robot navigation in dynamic, human-centered environments requires socially-compliant decisions grounded in robust scene understanding. Recent Vision-Language Models (VLMs) exhibit promising capabilities such as object recognition, common-sense reasoning, and contextual understanding, capabilities that align with the nuanced requirements of social robot navigation. However, it remains unclear whether VLMs can accurately understand complex social navigation scenes (e.g., inferring the spatial-temporal relations among agents and human intentions), which is essential for safe and socially compliant robot navigation. While some recent works have explored the use of VLMs in social robot navigation, no existing work systematically evaluates their ability to meet these necessary conditions. In this paper, we introduce the Social Navigation Scene Understanding Benchmark (SocialNav-SUB), a Visual Question Answering (VQA) dataset and benchmark designed to evaluate VLMs for scene understanding in real-world social robot navigation scenarios. SocialNav-SUB provides a unified framework for evaluating VLMs against human and rule-based baselines across VQA tasks requiring spatial, spatiotemporal, and social reasoning in social robot navigation. Through experiments with state-of-the-art VLMs, we find that while the best-performing VLM achieves an encouraging probability of agreeing with human answers, it still underperforms simpler rule-based approach and human consensus baselines, indicating critical gaps in social scene understanding of current VLMs. Our benchmark sets the stage for further research on foundation models for social robot navigation, offering a framework to explore how VLMs can be tailored to meet real-world social robot navigation needs. An overview of this paper along with the code and data can be found at this https URL .

</details>


### [116] [PrefPI: Preference-Guided Steering into Out-of-Distribution Behaviors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.40165)
*Seungeun Rho,Wontaek Kim,Danfei Xu,Sehoon Ha*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: PrefPI 利用对自生成轨迹的相对偏好，迭代引导预训练机器人策略，推动其产生初始策略中罕见或未出现的行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有偏好学习方法往往只能强化策略已能生成的行为模式，难以引导策略超出初始有效支持范围。

Method: 将偏好学习建模为偏好条件生成：用偏好轨迹定义条件分布，并通过其与行为先验的密度比提取隐式偏好信号，再以无分类器引导（CFG）放大该信号。重复条件建模与引导，形成偏好引导的策略迭代。

Result: 在仿真和真实环境中，PrefPI 应用于扩散策略及 PI0.5 流匹配视觉—语言—动作模型，少量反馈即可带来显著行为变化。真实硬件上的物体搬运高度从 10.7 厘米提升至 19.8 厘米，仅使用 150 条带偏好标签的轨迹。

Conclusion: 偏好条件生成与迭代引导能够突破初始策略的行为覆盖限制，以有限偏好反馈推动机器人策略逐步获得此前难以实现的行为。

Abstract: We present PrefPI (Preference-Guided Policy Iteration), an iterative framework for steering pretrained generative robot policies using only relative preferences over self-generated trajectories. Unlike prior preference-learning methods that primarily sharpen modes already represented by the policy, we study steering beyond the initial effective support, where desired behaviors are rarely or never observed under the initial policy. Our key idea is to formulate preference learning as preference-conditioned generative modeling: preferred trajectories define a conditional distribution, whose density ratio with the broader behavior prior provides an implicit preference signal amplified by classifier-free guidance (CFG). Repeating this preference-conditioned modeling and guidance step yields a form of preference-guided policy iteration, turning incremental improvements toward previously inaccessible behaviors. Across diffusion policies and the PI0.5 flow- matching VLA in simulation and the real world, PrefPI produces substantial behavioral shifts with limited feedback. In particular, PrefPI increases object transport height from 10.7 cm to 19.8 cm on real hardware with only 150 preference-labeled trajectories.

</details>


### [117] [BatSLAM 2.0: Sequence-Verified Sonar Place Recognition in a Robust Pose Graph](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.40085)
*Jan Steckel*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 论文提出 BatSLAM 2.0，一种仅依靠声呐的 SLAM 系统，可稳健构建拓扑地图并减少错误闭环导致的地图坍塌。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 声呐地点识别具有天然歧义：不同走廊可能产生近似的回声序列，错误闭环会使拓扑地图失效。

Method: 系统由三部分构成：更新后的声学前端、用于跟踪并验证闭环候选的序列验证器，以及基于高性能因子图框架实现的位姿图。

Result: 在仿真数据和真实录音上的评估表明，该系统能够稳健构建拓扑地图、抵御地图坍塌，并支持地图规模扩展。

Conclusion: BatSLAM 2.0 通过改进声学处理、闭环验证与图优化，为仅依靠声呐的稳健 SLAM 提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Echolocating bats can navigate dark and cluttered spaces using echolocation. Over a decade ago, BatSLAM showed that a robot with a biomimetic binaural sonar can build a topological map of the environment, by recognizing places from the received acoustic signals. Sonar place recognition, however, is ambiguous by nature: corridors produce nearly identical echo trains, and wrong loop closure can collapse the topological map. In this paper, we introduce BatSLAM 2.0, a novel sonar-only SLAM system built from three elements: an updated acoustic front-end, a sequence verifier that tracks and verifies loop closure candidates and a pose graph implemented on a high performance factor graph framework. The system was thoroughly evaluated both in simulated as well as real world recordings. In both cases, the BatSLAM2.0 algorithm shows the capability of robust topological map creation, countering map collapse, and robust scaling of map size.

</details>


### [118] [When Instructions Retrieve Trajectories: Diagnosing and Mitigating Generalization Failures in VLA Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39971)
*Hung-Jen Chen,Yu-Hsun Hou,Yan-Hong Chen,Yan-Fu Chen,Binghua Cai,Min Sun,Chun-Yi Lee*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文指出，视觉—语言—动作模型可能同时利用语言和视觉，却未能将指令与场景正确绑定以选择所需动作；提出等变反事实训练（ECT），在模拟与真实机器人任务中显著提升反事实位置变化下的成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 常见鲁棒性指标可能掩盖一种关键失效：模型在分布内任务表现良好，也能应对不改变所需动作的视觉扰动，但面对要求改变动作的反事实场景时失败。研究将此问题称为“指令—动作绑定”失效。

Method: 分析微调后的π₀.₅与GR00T-N1.7策略的行为、内部状态读出与干预，并考察模仿学习目标的作用机制。基于分析，提出ECT：数据层面提供同一指令在可区分场景中对应不同动作的有效示范；损失层面则在同一次更新中联合训练一组对应示范。

Result: 失败轨迹常保留原有行为，或转向另一项已示范任务，表明模型并非单纯忽略语言。受控LIBERO-PRO比较中，完整ECT将π₀.₅的位置交换平均成功率从36%提高至59%；在原始数据已有对应示范的CALVIN上，仅使用ECT损失即可提升五任务完成表现；固定示范预算的真实UR5e实验中，未见位置成功率从8%升至88%。

Conclusion: 狭窄的条件动作支持可能使基于场景的动作解法与由指令触发的解法在示范数据上难以区分。ECT通过构造并联合训练反事实对应示范，增强模型将语言指令与视觉场景绑定、据此选择正确动作的能力。

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) models can exceed 90% success on in-distribution tasks and withstand nuisance changes that preserve the required action, yet fail under counterfactual changes that demand a different action. Aggregate robustness scores can therefore conceal a more specific failure, in which a policy responds to both language and vision yet does not combine them to select the action the task requires. We call this failure instruction-action binding. Instructions cue familiar trajectory families, and visual feedback adjusts their execution. Behavioral analyses of fine-tuned $\pi_{0.5}$ and GR00T-N1.7 policies reveal that failed rollouts often retain the source behavior or switch to another demonstrated task. These switches show that language is not simply ignored. Readouts and interventions connect these choices to task-conditioned internal states. Our analysis of the imitation objective shows how narrow conditional action support can leave grounded and instruction-keyed solutions indistinguishable on the demonstrations. This motivates Equivariant Counterfactual Training (ECT), which acts at two levels. ECT data supply valid demonstrations in which the same instruction requires different actions in distinguishable scenes, while the ECT loss trains each demonstration with its counterpart in the same update. In a controlled LIBERO-PRO comparison, full ECT raises $\pi_{0.5}$'s mean position-swap success from 36% to 59%. On CALVIN, where counterparts already occur in the original data, the ECT loss improves five-task completion without new demonstrations. On a real UR5e under a fixed demonstration budget, full ECT raises unseen-position success from 8% to 88%.

</details>


### [119] [General Performance Guarantee for Human Torque Estimation-Based Task-Agnostic Assistive Exoskeleton Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39558)
*Duy Hoang,Bastien Berret,Olivier Bruneau,Laurent Fribourg*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种面向外骨骼的交互力矩设计方法，在力矩分布范围内保证辅助与人体运动相匹配的概率下界，并通过多任务实验验证其效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人体力矩估计误差可能导致机器人辅助与人体意图不一致，降低外骨骼的可控性和任务表现，因此需要可靠、具备泛化能力的辅助策略。

Method: 将“匹配辅助”形式化定义为机器人对人体运动产生正向贡献，并据此建立理论框架，设计期望交互力矩，使匹配辅助概率具有可保证的下界。该保证覆盖整个力矩分布，包括训练任务之外的未见数据。

Result: 研究在 ABLE 上肢外骨骼上开展多任务评估。实验验证了理论保证，并显示该策略能在多项任务中实现有效辅助、保持运动平顺，同时降低人体体力负担。

Conclusion: 该方法为外骨骼交互力矩设计提供了具有理论保证的方案，并展现出跨任务可靠性与泛化潜力。

Abstract: Accurate human torque estimation is crucial for enabling task-agnostic control in robotic exoskeleton systems. However, estimation errors may cause mismatches between the robot assistance and the human intention, degrading controllability and task performance. In this paper, we address this issue by formally defining matched assistance as scenarios in which the robot positively contributes to human movement. Based on this definition, we develop a theoretical framework to design the robot's desired interaction torque that guarantees a lower bound on the matched assistance probability. Importantly, the proposed guarantee holds over the entire torque distribution, including unseen data beyond the training tasks. This provides our method with strong reliability and generalization, both of which are critical for effective exoskeleton control. The proposed strategy is implemented on the ABLE upper-limb exoskeleton and evaluated in a multi-task setup. Experimental results validate the theoretical guarantees and demonstrate that the proposed strategy achieves effective general performance across several tasks, guaranteeing movement smoothness while reducing human physical effort.

</details>


### [120] [MotionWeave: Learning Motion-Centered Future Dynamics for Vision-Language-Action Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39324)
*Jingqiu Wang,Yan Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: MotionWeave是一种面向视觉-语言-动作模型的运动中心未来动力学框架，通过动作条件下的局部交互区域定位与跨时域运动残差建模，提升动作分块预测性能。在六项MetaWorld任务上平均成功率达到75.3%，较π0提升8.6个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有世界模型通常预测未来图像或视频，其中包含与控制无关的外观变化；基于整体未来视觉表征和共享全局动作特征的引导，也难以建立动作时间步与局部视觉变化之间的精确对应关系。因此，需要一种关注局部交互区域、具有时间步特异性的运动建模方法。

Method: MotionWeave由Action-Induced Motion Grounder（AIMG）和Horizon Residual Composer（HRC）组成。AIMG结合动作表征与本体感知表征生成面向不同预测时域的查询，并从当前视觉标记中定位与各未来动作时间步相关的交互区域。HRC计算相邻时域交互表征之间的差异，将其编码为时间运动线索，并通过门控残差注入动作标记。训练阶段利用未来帧渲染的机械臂掩码构造基于KL散度的运动定位监督；推理阶段仅使用当前观测。

Result: 在六项MetaWorld任务上，MotionWeave取得75.3%的平均成功率，相比π0的66.7%提高8.6个百分点。性能提升在持续交互类任务上尤为明显，表明局部运动线索和时域残差建模具有较高有效性。

Conclusion: 该方法通过将动作条件的局部交互定位与跨时域运动变化建模相结合，缓解了未来视觉预测中的控制无关信息和动作—视觉时间对应不足问题，为VLA模型的动作分块预测提供了更具针对性的动态监督。结果显示，运动中心、时间步特异的表征能够有效提升机器人控制性能。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models have recently incorporated world models to provide richer dynamic supervision beyond sparse action labels. However, explicitly predicting future images or videos may include control-irrelevant appearance, while guidance derived from holistic future visual representations and shared global action features may fail to establish timestep-specific correspondence between actions and local visual changes. To address this issue, we propose MotionWeave, a motion-centric future-dynamics framework for action-chunk prediction with two modules: the Action-Induced Motion Grounder (AIMG) and the Horizon Residual Composer (HRC). Specifically, AIMG conditions on action and proprioceptive representations to construct horizon-specific queries that localize interaction regions associated with each future action timestep from current visual tokens. HRC extracts differences between interaction representations at adjacent horizons, encodes them as temporal motion cues, and injects them into action tokens through a gated residual. During training, robot-arm masks rendered from future frames are used to construct KL-based motion-grounding supervision, while inference uses only the current observation. On six MetaWorld tasks, MotionWeave achieves a 75.3% average success rate, an absolute gain of 8.6% over {\pi}0 (66.7%), especially on sustained-interaction tasks. Our code is available at this https URL .

</details>


### [121] [Linear Recurrent Memory Suffices to Distil a World-Model Policy for Robot Air Hockey](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39151)
*F. Olivia Fan,Oliver Obst*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 在模拟冰球防守的追踪中断任务中，低维线性循环记忆策略与 GRU 和教师策略表现相当；增加非线性循环创新未带来可测收益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究记忆依赖型控制是否必须采用非线性循环动力学，还是简单的线性记忆机制已足够。

Method: 在模拟冰球防守中暂时中断冰球追踪。先比较 DreamerV3 教师策略与无记忆策略，并通过重置教师的循环状态检验记忆需求；再将教师蒸馏为具有 64 维状态的紧凑循环策略，比较对角线性循环（k=0）与加入不同秩非线性创新的模型，并以 GRU 为基线，在五个匹配随机种子下评测。

Result: 教师策略优于无记忆策略，重置其循环状态会显著降低表现，表明任务需要记忆。纯线性循环模型在所测试的追踪中断范围内与 GRU 和教师表现相当；提高非线性创新秩没有带来可测收益。线性模型的循环参数更少、计算量更低。

Conclusion: 对于该任务及所考察的设置，非线性观测编码器和动作输出头配合简单线性记忆机制即可实现有效控制，非线性循环动力学并非必需。结论仅适用于所用模拟任务、教师、状态维度、中断时长及保持非线性的编码器和动作头。

Abstract: Does memory-dependent control need nonlinear recurrent dynamics? We study simulated air-hockey defence under temporary loss of puck tracking. A DreamerV3 teacher outperforms a memoryless policy under tracking loss, while resetting the teacher's recurrent state sharply reduces performance, which demonstrates that the task requires memory. We distil this teacher into compact recurrent policies with a 64 dimensional state, with a combination of a diagonal linear recurrence and an optional rank-$k$ nonlinear innovation while retaining nonlinear observation encoders and action heads. Across five matched seeds, the purely linear recurrent model ($k=0$) matches both the GRU baseline and the teacher throughout the tested range of tracking loss. Increasing nonlinear innovation rank providing no measured benefits. This result is obtained on a fresh test split, which will be only opened after all models and analyses are frozen. The linear model requires fewer recurrent parameters and less computation than GRU, but performs comparably. These results suggest that, for this memory dependent control task, nonlinear representation learning around a simple linear memory mechanism can be sufficient, and that nonlinear recurrent dynamics are not necessarily required. These conclusions are limited to the simulated task, teacher, state dimension, and blackout horizon considered here, and to policies whose observation encoder and action head remain nonlinear.

</details>


### [122] [SimEX: Simulation-Integrated Robotics AutoResearch](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38982)
*Jiaheng Hu,Roberto Martin-Martin,Peter Stone,Rocky Duan,Zhenyu Jiang,Guanya Shi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: SimEX 将模拟实验与少量真实机器人试验结合，使编码智能体能够高效获得机器人操作技能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 直接生成控制程序可能受限于智能体对机器人及物理环境理解不足；在真实世界反复试错则成本高且存在安全顾虑。

Method: 分两阶段运行：先在模拟环境中反复探测和优化，构建稳健、可泛化的机器人工具箱；再通过少量真实试验同步校正工具箱与模拟器，并利用校正后的模拟器诊断失败、筛选修复方案。

Result: 研究通过仿真到仿真实验及实体机器人任务进行评估。在毛巾折叠、条码扫描和盘子操作等任务中，智能体无需示范，仅用约10分钟真实机器人交互即可获得操作技能。

Conclusion: 模拟器不仅可提供训练数据，也可作为近似真实的实验环境，帮助智能体形成并改进实体机器人所需的知识与操作流程。

Abstract: Coding agents powered by large language models (LLMs) have shown remarkable abilities to autonomously reason about and achieve goals in the digital world. However, bringing this success to the physical world remains challenging. On the one hand, direct generation methods (e.g., Code as Policies) often suffer from the LLMs' insufficient understanding of robots and physical environments. On the other hand, iterative trial-and-error tuning in the physical world (e.g., physical autoresearch) induces significant experimental cost and safety concerns. We introduce SimEX: Simulation-Integrated Robotics AutoResearch, an autoresearch framework that tightly integrates simulated experimentation, enabling coding agents to efficiently acquire physical capabilities for controlling real robots. SimEX operates in two stages. First, the agent conducts open-ended probe-and-optimize iterations in simulation, developing a robot toolbox with robust and generalizable capabilities. Second, the agent adapts the toolbox and the simulator together through only a few physical trials: each trial corrects the simulator, and the corrected simulator is used to diagnose failures and screen candidate repairs. We evaluate SimEX extensively in sim-to-sim settings and on physical robots. On challenging real-world manipulation tasks including towel folding, barcode scanning, and plate manipulation, SimEX enables coding agents to efficiently acquire robot skills without any demonstration and with only 10 minutes of real-robot interaction. These results suggest that simulation can be a critical component in achieving physical intelligence, not only as a source of training data that must closely replicate the real world, but also as a roughly correct laboratory where a coding agent develops the knowledge and procedures needed to act on the robot. More details and robot videos at this https URL

</details>


### [123] [TERRA: Terrain-Aware Reconstruction, Retargeting and Control for Musculoskeletal Locomotion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38653)
*Merkourios Simos,Chengkun Li,Bianca Ziliotto,Alexander Mathis*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: TERRA 是一套端到端流程，可从缺少场景信息的运动轨迹中恢复地形支撑几何，并训练肌肉驱动智能体在多种非平坦地形上行走。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有肌肉驱动运动控制主要局限于平地：运动数据通常缺少与动作对齐的地形几何，而将地形交互迁移到复杂肌肉骨骼模型也较困难。

Method: TERRA 仅以运动学轨迹为输入，结合地形先验、估计接触信息和自由空间的负证据，恢复与任务相关的支撑几何；重定向时纳入解剖结构、肌腱连续性及接触约束，并使用五个数据集生成的运动—地形配对数据训练控制策略。

Result: 研究使用总计 9.4 小时的多样化运动训练单一肌肉驱动策略。在重建、重定向和留出轨迹跟踪测试中，TERRA 提升了地形精度，大幅减少解剖与交互约束违规，并在所支持的地形类别中取得最高的观测任务完成率。

Conclusion: TERRA 为将不含场景信息的运动数据转化为适用于多种非平坦地形的肌肉驱动运动控制，提供了实用方案。

Abstract: Recent advances in musculoskeletal modeling and reinforcement learning have enabled muscle-actuated agents to reproduce increasingly complex human motions. Yet these capabilities remain largely confined to flat ground, in part because motion datasets rarely include aligned terrain geometry and because retargeting terrain interactions to complex musculoskeletal bodies is challenging. We present TERRA, an end-to-end pipeline for terrain-aware retargeting and control of musculoskeletal locomotion. From kinematic trajectories alone, TERRA combines terrain priors, estimated contacts, and negative free-space evidence to recover task-relevant support geometry. TERRA further considers anatomical, tendon-continuity, and contact constraints during retargeting. Using the resulting motion-terrain pairs from five datasets, we successfully train a single muscle-actuated control policy on 9.4 hours of diverse locomotion. Across reconstruction, retargeting, and held-out tracking benchmarks, TERRA improves terrain accuracy, sharply reduces anatomical and interaction violations, and achieves the highest observed completion rate over supported terrain families. Overall, TERRA provides a practical route from scene-less motion data to muscle-actuated locomotion over diverse non-flat terrain. Project website: this https URL

</details>


### [124] [Correcting WHERE, Preserving HOW: Compositional Generalization for Vision-Language-Action Models via Referential Guidance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38616)
*Yanyan Zhang,Disheng Liu,Xinpeng Li,Chaoda Song,Mohsen Hariri,Debargha Ganguly,Wang Yang,Kai Ye,Bryce Grant,Vipin Chaudhary,Yu Yin*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: ReGuide 是一种免训练的 VLA 包装方法，通过语义与几何重绑定，帮助策略应对物体、目标位置和背景的组合变化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA 训练数据多样性不足，容易依赖与任务无关的视觉线索，导致已见元素重新组合时泛化受限。现有方法缺少显式的未见组合处理机制，且往往需要针对骨干模型修改并重新训练。

Method: 利用 grounding 模块提供的物体位姿，ReGuide 将指令中的语义指代与几何信息重新绑定，引导末端执行器进入训练示范支持的配置；随后由冻结的 VLA 策略继续执行。该方法利用策略在熟悉配置附近仍保留的局部操作能力，无需训练或修改骨干模型。

Result: 仿真中，ReGuide 在多个 VLA 骨干模型上的组合变化任务中，成功率最高提升 56.8 个百分点；真实机器人实验中最高提升 75.0 个百分点，同时保持标准任务表现。

Conclusion: ReGuide 为 VLA 的组合泛化提供了一种免训练、可适配多种骨干模型的补充方案，通过改善全局指代定位来发挥策略已有的局部操作能力。

Abstract: While Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models enable flexible action generation, their generalization across diverse environmental elements, including manipulated objects, destinations, and backgrounds, is limited by the lack of diversity in robotic training data. Trained end-to-end on such data, VLAs tend to exploit visual shortcuts, associating actions with task-irrelevant visual features rather than the intended task semantics. These shortcuts block recomposition of elements already seen by the policy, that is, compositional generalization. Existing approaches mitigate such entanglement through task-relevant perception or targeted data diversification, but offer no explicit mechanism for unseen recomposition and require backbone-specific modifications with retraining. We observe that under such recomposition, VLAs often fail at global grounding while retaining local manipulation skills that recover near the correct target in familiar configurations. Therefore, we propose Referential Guidance (ReGuide), a training-free wrapper that, given object poses from a grounding module, combines semantic and geometric rebinding to guide the end-effector into demonstration-supported configurations of the instructed referent, where the frozen policy can resume execution. Experiments in simulation across multiple VLA backbones as well as on a real robot show that ReGuide improves success rates under compositional shifts by up to 56.8 and 75.0 percentage points, respectively, while preserving standard-task performance.

</details>


### [125] [Diffusion-2BC: Hybrid Diffusion and Regression Training for Offline Behavior Cloning in Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38472)
*Bruno Maciel Machado,Eric Aislan Antonelo*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Diffusion-2BC 在扩散式行为克隆训练中加入辅助确定性克隆损失，提升闭环表现，同时保留多模态动作预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 均方误差回归难以适配同一观测对应多种有效动作的示范数据；扩散策略虽能建模多模态动作，但在视觉与控制数据有限时，闭环表现可能不稳定。

Method: 使用共享视觉编码器，同时训练扩散去噪目标和辅助确定性行为克隆损失；辅助分支仅用于训练，推理阶段仍采用扩散策略。方法在 Claw 控制任务及 CARLA 鸟瞰导航中评估，覆盖路线条件驾驶、无路线多路口导航和跨地图测试。

Result: 在 Claw 中，Diffusion-2BC 的平均掩码距离误差较扩散行为克隆基线降低约10%，较标准确定性行为克隆降低约85%。在 Town01 和 Town02 的无路线 CARLA 测试中，其终止前行驶距离均显著超过两个基线。定性结果还显示智能体会选择不同路线。

Conclusion: 辅助回归信号有助于提升扩散式行为克隆的闭环可靠性，且不必牺牲多模态预测能力。现有证据来自受控任务与特定 CARLA 评估设置，结论的适用范围仍需进一步验证。

Abstract: Behavior cloning provides an offline route to autonomous-driving policy learning, but mean-squared-error regression is poorly matched to demonstrations in which one observation admits several valid actions. Diffusion policies can represent conditional multimodal action distributions, yet their closed-loop performance may be unstable when visual features and control are learned from limited data. This paper presents Diffusion-2BC, which combines a diffusion denoising objective with an auxiliary deterministic behavior-cloning loss over a shared visual encoder. The auxiliary branch is used only during training; inference remains diffusion-based. The proposed method is evaluated in the controlled Claw environment and in bird's-eye-view CARLA navigation, including route-conditioned driving, route-free navigation through multiple intersections, and cross-map evaluation from Town01 to Town02. In the Claw task, Diffusion-2BC reduced the mean mask-distance error by approximately 10% relative to a diffusion-based behavior-cloning baseline and by 85% relative to standard deterministic behavior cloning. In route-free CARLA, Diffusion-2BC traveled substantially farther before termination under the evaluation protocol than both baselines in Town01 and Town02. Additional qualitative rollouts revealed distinct route choices, showing the multimodal behavior of the proposed diffusion-based agent. The results indicate that an auxiliary regression signal can improve the closed-loop reliability of diffusion behavior cloning while preserving multimodal prediction in the controlled benchmark.

</details>


### [126] [GestAdapt: Workspace-Conditioned Co-Speech Gesture Generation for Humanoid Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38400)
*Bosong Ding,Xianglin Zhang,Miao Xin,Murat Kirtay,Giacomo Spigler*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: GestAdapt 在生成阶段将手腕可用空间作为条件，生成适应工作区的机器人伴随语音手势；实验结果优于忽略工作区或事后约束轨迹的方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人手势需同时适应语音、身体形态和可用工作空间。事后修正不受约束的动作可能损害自然度，因此需要在生成过程中纳入空间限制。

Method: 提出 GestAdapt 框架，以指定的手腕工作空间为条件生成伴随语音手势；利用六个互补语料，通过共享运动表示学习，并支持向不同机器人形态重定向。评估包括定量分析、用户研究和 Reachy2 实机比较。

Result: 生成动作在遵守工作空间限制的同时，整体接近真实动作分布。用户研究中，受限工作空间下生成的手势平均评分为 3.24/5，高于无工作空间条件的 2.43/5，但低于参考动作的 3.68/5。实机比较中，GestAdapt 动作在 69.7% 的比较中排名第一，优于无工作空间基线及事后施加空间约束的重定向真实动作。

Conclusion: 结果支持在手势生成阶段适应可用工作空间，而不是生成后再修正轨迹；前者更有利于兼顾空间可行性与手势自然度。

Abstract: Co-speech gestures for robots must adapt not only to speech and embodiment, but also to the workspace available for performing the motion. Since the same speech can be accompanied by different gestures, a robot can respond to workspace constraints, e.g., gestures for speech next to a wall. In these scenarios, the robot should gesture in a suitable motion rather than simply correcting an unconstrained one. To achieve this goal, we present GestAdapt, a workspace-conditioned framework that conditions co-speech gesture generation on a prescribed wrist workspace. The GestAdapt framework learns from six complementary co-speech corpora through a shared motion representation and supports retargeting to different robot embodiments. Quantitative evaluation shows that generated motions remain close to the real-motion distribution while respecting the workspace. In a user study, gestures generated under modified workspace constraints receive a mean quality score of 3.24/5, above our no-workspace variant (2.43/5) and below the reference motions (3.68/5). In a real robot evaluation, all compared motions are retargeted to the Reachy2 humanoid robot under identical workspace constraints. Motions generated with our framework rank first in 69.7\% of comparisons, higher than our no-workspace variant baseline and retargeted ground-truth motions constrained afterward. Overall, the results support adapting gestures to the available workspace during generation, rather than modifying unconstrained trajectories afterward to satisfy workspace constraints, potentially compromising gesture naturalness.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [127] [WorldAuditBench: Interactive 3D World Auditing with Multimodal Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.40325)
*Ziyan Jiang,Jingbo Yang,Jiabao Ji,Yujian Liu,Qiucheng Wu,Tommi Jaakkola,Yang Zhang,Shiyu Chang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出 WorldAuditBench，用于评测多模态智能体在交互式 3D 环境中探索并识别异常的能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 3D 世界审计既需要智能体系统地采取行动、搜寻异常，也需要其理解视觉信息并判断异常。多模态智能体能否有效结合这两种能力，仍缺乏系统研究。

Method: 构建包含 13 个环境、213 项异常任务的基准，覆盖五类异常；在固定探索预算下评测五种前沿模型，并比较两种范式：先由 VLA 探索、再由 VLM 识别异常；以及由 VLM 端到端地根据视觉推理选择行动并识别异常。

Result: 模型在两种范式下的成功率为 6.6% 至 42.3%，明显低于人类的 83.4%。结果显示，模型在探索过程中收集和解读证据方面仍存在显著不足。

Conclusion: WorldAuditBench 为研究多模态智能体如何在交互式 3D 环境中结合行动与视觉推理提供了测试平台，也揭示了当前模型在 3D 世界审计任务中的能力局限。

Abstract: As interactive 3D worlds are increasingly used to study intelligent behavior, it becomes important to develop efficient pipelines for identifying anomalies in these simulated environments, such as floating objects, traversable walls, or objects inconsistent with the surrounding scene. Multimodal AI systems, including vision-language models (VLMs) and vision-language-action models (VLAs), have shown potential for automating this task. However, 3D world auditing is complex, requiring the close coupling of two distinct capabilities: action, to navigate the 3D world and search for anomalies systematically and efficiently; and visual reasoning, to understand the environment and identify anomalies from multimodal observations. It remains largely unexplored whether multimodal agents can effectively couple these two capabilities, using visual reasoning to identify potential anomalies while taking actions to validate them. In this paper, we introduce WorldAuditBench, a benchmark for 3D world auditing comprising 213 anomaly tasks across 13 environments built with Unreal Engine 5 and this http URL , spanning five anomaly families. We evaluate five frontier models under a fixed exploration budget using two auditing paradigms: VLA-based exploration followed by VLM-based anomaly identification, and an end-to-end VLM agent in which visual reasoning directly guides action selection. Across the evaluated models and two paradigms, success rates range from 6.6% to 42.3%, substantially below human performance (83.4%). Through the task of world auditing, WorldAuditBench provides a testbed for studying how multimodal agents couple action and visual reasoning in interactive 3D environments, while highlighting current limitations in their ability to gather and interpret evidence during exploration.

</details>


### [128] [PivotOPD: Learning to Recover from Pivotal Mistakes in Multi-Turn Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.40285)
*Yinghui He,Yapei Chang,Khushi Bhardwaj,Daniele Molinari,Tugrul Konuk,Jan Kautz,Ali Hatamizadeh*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: PivotOPD通过同时训练模型避免关键错误并从错误状态中恢复，提升了多轮语言智能体的任务表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多轮交互中的错误动作会改变后续状态，使错误逐步累积。实验发现，失败轨迹中超过一半包含通常较早出现的关键错误；但这些错误往往仍可挽回，短暂引导即可恢复成功。

Method: 在关键错误处，教师模型提供正确动作，并为之后若干轮指定恢复动作。预防性蒸馏采用反向KL，促使学生避免关键错误；恢复性蒸馏采用正向KL，将学生较少采样到的恢复行为传授给学生。

Result: 在ALFWorld、WebShop和基于搜索的问答任务中，PivotOPD在Qwen3-1.7B和Qwen3-8B学生模型上，相较13种基线取得最强平均表现；在ALFWorld上，1.7B模型较最强基线提升5.5%。迁移至软件工程任务后，Nemotron-3.5在SWE-Bench Verified上的解决率提升3.2%。

Conclusion: 针对关键错误同时进行预防与恢复训练，有助于缓解多轮交互中的错误累积，并能提升不同模型规模及模型家族的任务表现。

Abstract: On-policy distillation (OPD) is a promising approach for training language agents, providing dense teacher supervision on student-generated trajectories. However, in multi-turn interaction, an incorrect action changes the states the student encounters later, so errors compound across turns. In preliminary experiments across three Qwen3 models (8B to 235B), we find that more than half of the failed rollouts contain a pivotal mistake, an action that moves the agent farther from completing the task, and this mistake typically occurs early. These pivotal mistakes often remain recoverable: guiding the model for only a few turns after the pivotal turn can restore task success. We therefore propose PivotOPD, an on-policy distillation framework that jointly trains the student to prevent pivotal mistakes and to recover from the states they create. At each pivotal mistake, a teacher model provides a gold action and then names a recovery action at each of the next few turns. Preventive distillation uses the gold action with reverse KL to steer the student away from the pivotal mistake, while recovery distillation uses the recovery actions with forward KL to transfer recovery behaviors that the student rarely samples. Against 13 baselines on ALFWorld, WebShop, and Search-based QA, PivotOPD achieves the strongest average performance for both Qwen3-1.7B and Qwen3-8B students, improving over the strongest baseline on ALFWorld by +5.5% with the 1.7B student. The gains also transfer to another model family on the software engineering domain, where PivotOPD raises the resolve rate of a Nemotron-3.5 student on SWE-Bench Verified by +3.2%. Project page: this https URL

</details>


### [129] [Belief-Aware Multi-Agent Path Finding under Map Uncertainty](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.40269)
*Viraj Parimi,Shao-Hung Chan,Han Zhang,Jingkai Chen,Brian Williams*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: MAGIC 利用空间相关性，从局部观测推断附近未知位置的可通行性，帮助多智能体路径规划减少执行代价。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统多智能体路径规划通常假设静态障碍已知；面对执行期间固定但事先未知的地图差异，既有方法主要依赖直接观测或重规划，未利用空间相关性推断未观测位置，可能导致后续绕行代价。

Method: 提出 MAGIC（Multi-Agent Gaussian belief Inference for Coordination），通过高斯马尔可夫随机场与高斯置信传播，根据智能体观测在线更新共享的可通行性信念，并据此构造考虑绕行的代价，供标准 MAPF 规划器使用。

Result: 在 MAPF 基准测试中，相较既有方法，MAGIC 在 96.3% 的测试实例上降低了执行总成本；实验涵盖多类规划器及最多 800 个智能体。

Conclusion: 利用空间相关信息进行协同信念推断，可提升存在未知地图差异时的路径规划效果，且 MAGIC 展现出扩展至大规模任务的适用性。

Abstract: Multi-Agent Path Finding (MAPF) aims to find collision-free paths for multiple agents in a shared environment. Classical MAPF assumes that all static obstacles are known in advance, but real-world environments can change unexpectedly due to fallen objects, spills, or other local disturbances. When such changes are spatially correlated, an observation can inform traversability estimates beyond the observed location. Prior approaches address uncertainty in traversability through contingent plans or replanning based on direct observations, but do not leverage this spatial dependence to infer the traversability of nearby unobserved locations. As a result, they cannot use one observation to anticipate nearby unobserved obstacles that may cause costly rerouting later. We focus on Belief-Aware MAPF, where map discrepancies are fixed during execution but initially unknown, and observations can be informative beyond the observed location. We propose Multi-Agent Gaussian belief Inference for Coordination (MAGIC), a framework that updates a shared belief about traversability online based on agents' observations. MAGIC uses a Gaussian Markov Random Field and Gaussian Belief Propagation to approximately infer traversability and construct detour-aware costs for standard MAPF planners. Our experiments on MAPF benchmarks show that MAGIC reduces the executed sum of costs compared to existing approaches on 96.3% of instances, across several planner families and teams of up to 800 agents, demonstrating its applicability to large-scale MAPF problems.

</details>


### [130] [ChronoGraph: Functional 4D Scene Graphs with Vision-Language Models for Interaction Understanding and Grounded Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39665)
*Chenyangguang Zhang,Malgorzata Gwiazda,Guanlong Jiao,Yuanchen Ju,Federico Tombari,Koushil Sreenath,Marc Pollefeys,Sunghwan Hong*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: ChronoGraph以统一的功能性4D场景图连接交互理解与空间规划，并通过图结构训练提升视觉语言模型在两项任务上的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 具身智能体需要理解过去的动作如何改变场景，并据此决定行动位置、预测后果及指导后续行为；现有交互理解与空间规划之间缺少统一表示。

Method: 提出ChronoGraph，将作用于可供性部件的动作与语义、几何状态变化关联，并以相同形式表示已观察和预期的状态转移。构建自动生成图标注问答数据的ChronoGraphBench；通过两阶段训练ChronoGraphVLM：先以图作为思维链进行监督微调，再开展直接奖励图属性与答案正确性的联合4D图强化学习。

Result: 在不同模型规模的实验中，ChronoGraphVLM均优于相应的预训练基线，并能零样本迁移至VLM4D。真实环境演示表明，基于场景图的规划和可供性定位可支持移动操作，且无需额外微调。

Conclusion: 统一的4D场景图表示有助于衔接交互理解与空间规划；以图结构监督和奖励模型，能够提升状态变化推理与行动规划能力，并展现现实机器人应用潜力。

Abstract: Embodied agents must determine where to act, anticipate the resulting scene changes, and interpret observed outcomes to guide subsequent actions. This requires connecting 4D interaction understanding, which explains how past actions changed the scene, with spatially grounded planning, which determines how and where to act toward a goal and anticipates the resulting scene changes. We introduce ChronoGraph, a functional 4D scene graph that links actions on affordance parts to semantic and geometric state changes. By representing observed and anticipated transitions in the same form, it provides a shared basis for understanding and planning. We construct ChronoGraphBench through an automatic data engine that converts human-interaction videos and simulated robot trajectories into graph-annotated questions for training and evaluating Vision-Language Models (VLMs) on both tasks. Using these annotations, we train ChronoGraphVLM by adapting pretrained VLMs in two stages. Graph-as-Chain-of-Thought supervised fine-tuning teaches the models to reconstruct observed transitions and predict future ones as graph traces before answering. Subsequent joint 4D graph reinforcement learning directly rewards graph properties and answer correctness. Experiments across model scales show improvements over the corresponding pretrained baselines and zero-shot transfer to VLM4D. Real-world demonstrations further show that graph-based planning and affordance grounding support mobile manipulation through existing robot skills without additional fine-tuning.

</details>


### [131] [AVERT-VLN: Abstention-aware Visual Error Recovery and Training for Vision-and-Language Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39579)
*Minrui Liu,Jingke Wang,Yuehao Huang,Hao Su,Jiajun Lv,Yukai Ma,Yong Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: AVERT-VLN通过在线偏航监测与人工辅助恢复，以及离线偏好学习，提升未见环境中的视觉语言导航表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 未见环境中的布局和视觉条件变化可能导致导航执行偏离指令。持续人工监督成本较高，因此需要能够选择性请求纠正、恢复任务并利用纠正经验改进策略的闭环方案。

Method: 提出AVERT-VLN框架，使用独立于导航决策生成的视觉语言监测器，根据指令、视觉历史和当前观测判断执行是否偏离。构建含2万条反事实风险轨迹的LOSTNAV数据集，并分阶段训练监测器：先用正常轨迹学习进度评估，再结合正常与风险轨迹学习语义偏离识别。运行时通过异步侧路监测并在判定为偏离且控制器接受时请求人工恢复；离线则采用轨迹锚定偏好学习，在共享决策上下文中构造偏好对，仅监督需要纠正的决策。

Result: 在人工辅助评测下，AVERT-VLN在R2R-CE和RxR-CE的val-unseen划分上分别达到76.2%和66.3%的成功率。相同的监测与人工辅助恢复接口也提升了三种受测导航架构的成功率。

Conclusion: 将独立在线监测、按需人工恢复与针对性离线偏好学习结合，可增强导航智能体在未见环境中的鲁棒性，并能适配多种导航架构。

Abstract: Deploying vision-and-language navigation (VLN) agents in unseen environments remains challenging because unfamiliar layouts and visual conditions can cause execution to go off track. Rather than relying on continuous human supervision, a practical strategy is to selectively request corrective guidance, recover the ongoing task, and reuse corrective interactions to improve subsequent navigation. We propose Abstention-aware Visual Error Recovery and Training for Vision-and-Language Navigation (AVERT-VLN), a closed-loop framework that uses a plug-in vision-language Monitor for online human-assisted recovery and offline preference learning. The Monitor operates separately from navigation decision generation and assesses instruction-execution consistency from the instruction, visual history, and current observation. To train the Monitor for deviation recognition, we construct LOSTNAV DATASET with 20K counterfactual risk trajectories and rule-based deviation labels. The Monitor is first fine-tuned on 40K normal trajectories to assess instruction progress and then jointly fine-tuned on normal and risk trajectories to recognize semantic deviations. At runtime, Asynchronous Sidecar Monitoring evaluates execution alongside the navigation model. When the controller accepts a LOST verdict, it suspends autonomous execution and requests human guidance for recovery. For offline policy improvement, Trajectory-Anchored Preference Learning converts deviation-associated failures into decision-level preference pairs under shared decision contexts, restricting supervision to the decisions targeted for correction. Under human-assisted evaluation, the full AVERT-VLN system achieves success rates of 76.2% and 66.3% on the val-unseen splits of R2R-CE and RxR-CE, respectively. The same monitoring and human-assisted recovery interface also improves success rates across the three evaluated navigation architectures.

</details>


### [132] [Divide and Collapse: MAPF-Collapse via Exact Decomposition into Independent Sub-Instances](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39559)
*Oren Salzman*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出一种用于多智能体路径规划后优化的分解式框架，可在保持可行性和解质量的同时减少冗余移动，并显著加快求解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法 Judgelight 对所有智能体联合构建整数线性规划，计算成本受完整问题规模影响；但实际需要联合推理的部分通常较小，许多智能体可独立处理。

Method: 识别需要协调运动的智能体，并据此将实例划分为子问题。对无需协调的部分，采用轻量级求解器；对需要协调的部分，采用更适合简单问题的 CBS 类求解器，并在复杂情形下回退至 Judgelight。

Result: 无需协调情形下，轻量级求解器比 Judgelight 快约 1,900 倍。整体框架保持与 Judgelight 相同的解质量；在全部测试基准上，逐实例速度提升中位数为 10.5 倍。

Conclusion: 利用问题中独立子问题占多数这一结构，可在不依赖商用整数线性规划求解器的情况下实现精确优化，并显著提升整体效率。

Abstract: In this work we study the problem of MAPFC, a post-optimization step for Multi-Agent Path Finding (MAPF) plans where we are given a feasible plan produced by a modern MAPF solver and are tasked with removing avoidable moves while preserving feasibility. This NP-hard problem naturally arises when using learning-based state-of-the-art (SOTA) solvers which construct plans that contain redundant moves that can be removed. Recently, Tang et al. presented Judgelight, which uses Integer Linear Programming (ILP) to solve MAPFC. Importantly, the ILP is constructed over all agents jointly, so its cost is governed by the full instance rather than by the small coupled residue that actually requires joint reasoning. Our key insight, motivating this work, is that MAPFC instances naturally decompose into independent sub-problems, most of which involve a single agent and can be solved without any inter-agent reasoning. To this end, we first identify which agents need to coordinate their motion and partition the instance into sub-problems accordingly. For the cases where no coordination is required, we introduce an extremely lightweight solver that is $\approx\!1{,}900\times$ faster than Judgelight. For cases where coordination is required, Judgelight can be used but we introduce an alternative CBS-like solver which is more efficient on easier problems. The resulting framework is exact, uses no commercial ILP solver, and matches Judgelight's quality while running substantially faster on the coordination-light majority of instances; on the coordination-heavy instances we propose a regime-aware hybrid planner that falls back to Judgelight. Over all benchmarks tested, this planner achieves a median $10.5\times$ per-instance speedup over Judgelight.

</details>


### [133] [SkillFM: Generating Skills for LLM Agents via Latent Flow Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39382)
*Zuming Zhang,Jie He,Yizhe Zhang,Jeff Z. Pan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: SkillFM 通过条件流匹配直接生成任务相关的文本技能，无需测试时检索技能库，并在部分评测任务上优于对比的向量技能方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文本技能方法常依赖人工整理的技能库，或依赖间接且延迟的强化学习反馈。研究旨在提供一种可直接生成任务条件技能的替代方案。

Method: 使用编码器将文本技能映射到连续潜空间，并通过改进的 MeanFlow 训练条件流模型。推理时，模型利用学习到的速度场进行单步潜变量采样，再由基于大语言模型的解码器生成文本指导，供冻结的下游智能体使用。

Result: 在具身任务、问答和网页购物任务上进行了评估。SkillFM 在 ALFWorld 和 Search-QA 上取得了所比较的基于向量的技能方法中的最佳总体表现。分析结果也表明，潜空间技能生成可作为基于检索的技能增强方案的有效替代。

Conclusion: SkillFM 展示了通过生成而非检索来提供任务相关文本技能的可行性；摘要报告了其在部分基准上的优势，但未提供具体数值或显著性检验结果。

Abstract: Textual skills provide reusable guidance for large language model agents, but existing approaches often rely on manually curated skill banks or reinforcement learning with indirect and delayed feedback. We introduce SkillFM (Skill Flow Matching), a generative framework that synthesizes task-conditioned textual skills directly without test-time skill retrieval. Our framework combines a codec for encoding and reconstructing textual skills in a continuous latent space with a conditional flow model trained using improved MeanFlow. At inference time, the learned velocity field enables single-step latent sampling, and an LLM-based decoder converts the sampled representation into textual guidance for a frozen downstream agent. We evaluate the framework on embodied tasks, question answering, and web shopping. On ALFWorld and Search-QA, our method achieves the best overall performance among the compared vector-based skill approaches. Our analyses further demonstrate that latent skill generation is an effective alternative to retrieval-based skill augmentation. Our code and training skill libraries are available at this https URL .

</details>


### [134] [Beyond the Remembered World: Predictive 4D Belief for Persistent Navigation in Evolving Worlds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39166)
*Mingjian Gao,Zhaocheng Li,Haoyang Huang,Wenqiao Zhang,Yingjie Niu,Hao Zhou,Chao Li,Juncheng Li,Siliang Tang,Yueting Zhuang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究目标在未被观察时持续移动的导航问题，提出 EvolvingNav，并构建 EvoWorld-Bench；仿真与真实机器人实验显示，该方法提升了导航成功率和搜索效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统空间记忆常假定目标位置保持不变，但目标可能在智能体导航期间移动，导致记忆位置失效。智能体需要在观察有限的情况下预测目标状态，并根据新视觉证据及时修正信念。

Method: EvolvingNav 基于带时间戳的三维物体历史，采用结构化的持续—迁移模型构建随时间变化的信念，并保留已知候选位置之外的概率质量。事件驱动滤波器随时间推进信念、预测目标在检查时刻的占据位置，并融合新的 RGB-D 证据；它利用经校准且依赖可见性的检测概率处理未发现目标的负证据，并跟踪证据以避免重复使用观测。冻结的零样本视觉语言控制器依据更新后的信念选择行动并重新规划。研究还提出基于人类活动轨迹的 EvoWorld-Bench，包含 54 个场景和 803,680 项任务。

Result: 在仿真和真实机器人实验中，EvolvingNav 相较于所评估的基线提高了导航成功率和搜索效率。配对实验表明，在时间模式可学习时收益最明显；消融实验则验证了保留不确定性和引入可见性感知证据的价值。

Conclusion: 对动态目标进行有效导航，需要显式表示位置不确定性、预测目标随时间的变化，并根据可见性合理利用正负视觉证据。EvolvingNav 将这些机制整合进导航流程，在所测试的环境中取得了更好的表现。

Abstract: Persistent spatial memory enables embodied agents to navigate familiar environments across repeated visits. However, targets may move while unobserved, including during navigation, making remembered locations unreliable by the time an agent arrives. Despite advances in memory retrieval and state prediction, accounting for continued hidden world evolution and revising beliefs under limited visibility remain challenging. We study Evolving-World Navigation, where agents infer target locations from intermittent observations, predict their states at inspection time, and revise beliefs using visual evidence. We propose EvolvingNav, which constructs a time-indexed belief from timestamped 3D object histories through a structured persistence-relocation model. The belief distinguishes persistence at the last observed location from relocation to alternative locations and retains probability mass outside the known candidate set. An event-driven filter propagates the current belief as time elapses, forecasts target occupancy at candidate inspection times, and incorporates new RGB-D evidence. Negative observations downweight location hypotheses according to calibrated, visibility-conditioned detection probabilities, while evidence tracking prevents repeated use of the same observations. A frozen, zero-shot vision-language controller uses the updated belief to choose actions and replan. We further introduce EvoWorld-Bench, a benchmark grounded in human activity traces, comprising 54 scenes and 803,680 tasks with controlled changes before and during navigation. In simulation and real-robot experiments, EvolvingNav improves navigation success and search efficiency over the evaluated baselines. Paired experiments show the clearest gains under learnable temporal patterns, while ablations demonstrate the value of preserving uncertainty and incorporating visibility-aware evidence.

</details>


### [135] [Consistent Plan-Act for Long-Horizon Agentic Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38891)
*Heng-Zhuang Li,Yi-Kai Zhang,Yu Wang,Yueqing Sun,Jiayuan Zhang,Qi Gu,Han-Jia Ye*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文研究长时程智能体任务中的规划器—执行器状态不一致问题，并提出 ConPAct，通过矛盾反馈与一致性交互微调提升协作表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 规划器与执行器分工可提升任务处理能力，但二者可能对任务相关状态事实产生不同理解，导致协调失效。论文旨在识别这一问题并改善智能体协作。

Method: 要求规划器和执行器分别生成结构化状态断言，通过程序化比较检测明确矛盾；分析状态信息对不一致及任务表现的影响；提出 ConPAct，将检测到的矛盾反馈给双方以统一状态解释，并使用筛选后的一致性交互进行微调。

Result: 实验发现规划器与执行器对同一任务状态存在系统性分歧，提供任务相关状态信息可减少不一致并改善协调与表现。ConPAct 在多种环境和模型配置中提升了任务表现；例如，在 MiniGrid 中，成功率由 38.6% 提高至 54.4%。

Conclusion: 状态一致性是长时程智能体协作的重要因素。检测并纠正规划器—执行器之间的状态矛盾，既可用于推理阶段的即时修正，也可通过训练提升协调能力。

Abstract: Long-horizon agentic tasks demand strong reasoning and efficient execution across successive interactions with dynamic environments. A common approach decouples high-level planning from low-level execution through separate planner and actor roles. To investigate coordination failures in these tasks, we prompt both agents for structured state assertions and compare their reports programmatically to detect explicit contradictions. Our analyses reveal systematic disagreement about the same task-relevant state facts, a phenomenon we term planner-actor state mismatch. We further find that providing agents with task-relevant state information reduces mismatch and improves coordination and task performance. Based on the systematic analysis of the state mismatch, we propose Consistent Plan-Act (ConPAct), which feeds detected contradictions back to both agents to form consistent state interpretations and fine-tunes them on curated consistent interactions for better coordination. ConPAct improves performance across various environments and model configurations, e.g., increasing MiniGrid success rate from 38.6% to 54.4% with GPT-5.6-sol/terra as planner and actor respectively, demonstrating that state consistency can guide both inference-time correction and coordination training.

</details>


### [136] [Code to Control: Synthesizing Parameterized Reactive Controllers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38733)
*Zergham Ahmed,Joshua B. Tenenbaum,Chris Bates,Samuel J. Gershman*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Code to Control 将 LLM 生成的 Python 控制器结构与参数优化分离，训练完成后可直接执行策略，无需决策时调用 LLM 或进行规划。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于 LLM 的动作选择和需要逐步规划的世界模型可能带来较高延迟，限制实时控制应用。

Method: LLM 生成控制器程序结构；随后利用环境反馈，通过无导数搜索拟合连续控制任务所需的参数。训练完成后，控制器直接输出动作。

Result: 在 Atari、Flappy Bird 和 MuJoCo 任务中，该方法优于基于规划的程序合成方法；使用更少环境交互时仍能与深度强化学习保持竞争力，并能适应显著的环境动力学变化、扩展至复杂运动任务。按论文采用的计时协议，其动作选择速度快于 PPO 策略。

Conclusion: 将程序结构合成与参数搜索结合，可形成无需在线 LLM 推理或规划的低延迟控制器，展现出实时控制潜力；速度比较的结论依赖所采用的计时协议。

Abstract: Recent LLM-based approaches to control either invoke a language model to select actions or synthesize world models that require planning at every decision, introducing latency that can limit real-time use. We introduce Code to Control, an approach that synthesizes Python controllers which execute directly as policies. Code to Control separates program structure from parameters. An LLM synthesizes the controller structure, while derivative-free search fits its parameters for continuous control using feedback from the environment. Once learned, the resulting controllers require neither LLM inference nor planning at decision time, enabling real-time gameplay and, under our timing protocol, faster action selection than a PPO policy. Across a suite of Atari games, Flappy Bird, and MuJoCo tasks, Code to Control outperforms planning-based program synthesis methods, remains competitive with deep reinforcement learning while using fewer environment interactions, transfers across substantial changes in environment dynamics, and scales to complex locomotion tasks.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [137] [Learning to Plan from Random Exploration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38383)
*Deqian Kong,Guangyan Sun,Sheng Cheng,Sirui Xie,Bo Pang,Jianwen Xie,Tony Geng,Caiwen Ding,Ying Nian Wu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出一种无需策略改进训练的方法：从随机探索中学习多时间尺度的状态关系，并将其用于长程目标规划。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随机探索可在目标指定前积累环境经验。论文关注这些经验能否支持长程规划，而无需通过策略改进训练；关键在于刻画不同时间跨度下状态之间的关系。

Method: 通过随机游走分析时间关系：短时间跨度反映测地几何，较长跨度揭示区域连通性，直至混合效应抹去差异。模型采用按时间跨度条件化的嵌入，以噪声对比估计学习观测对的时间对数密度比，不需要动作或奖励标签。规划时，局部动力学模型预测候选动作结果，时间关系模型评估其朝目标的进展；智能体执行一步后重新规划，两个模型均保持固定。

Result: 实验表明，该方法可利用随机探索得到的状态或图像，在迷宫中进行长程规划；学习到的分数场、嵌入探测结果和规划路径呈现多尺度认知地图的特征。论文还展示了基于随机探索的第一人称导航，以及利用次优数据进行操作规划。

Conclusion: 随机探索中蕴含的多时间尺度关系能够支持长程规划，无需动作标签、奖励标签或策略改进训练。将这类关系模型与局部动力学模型结合，可形成执行一步、持续重规划的规划框架。

Abstract: Random exploration reveals how an environment can be traversed before a goal is specified. Can this experience support long-range planning without policy-improvement training? Our random-walk analysis explains what temporal relations contain: short horizons reveal geodesic geometry in the diffusion limit, while longer horizons reveal connectivity between regions before mixing removes these distinctions. We learn these relations with a conditional energy-based model that estimates temporal log-density ratios through horizon-conditioned embeddings. The model is trained on observation pairs by noise-contrastive estimation, without action or reward labels. The planner queries these learned relations at different horizons as it moves toward the goal. At test time, a separate local dynamics model predicts candidate action outcomes, and the temporal model evaluates their progress toward the goal by selecting or aggregating estimated improvements across horizons. The agent executes one action and replans with both models fixed. Experiments demonstrate long-range maze planning from random exploration using states and images. Learned score fields, embedding probes, and planned routes exhibit properties of a multiscale cognitive map. We further demonstrate egocentric navigation from random exploration and manipulation planning from suboptimal data.

</details>


### [138] [Role-Adaptive Policy Optimization for Offline Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.40149)
*Seonvin Cho,Soohyun Choi,Songnam Hong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出角色自适应策略优化（RAPO），根据策略在价值学习与动作执行中的不同作用，分别调整策略更新系数；在 D4RL 任务中，RAPO 优于对应基线，且对 TD3+BC 的提升更显著。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 离线强化学习需要在策略改进与避免依赖不确定价值估计之间权衡。用于价值函数自举的动作与实际执行的动作作用不同，但 TD3+BC 等方法用同一策略处理两者，限制了针对性调节。

Method: RAPO 通过对基于原算法演员目标构造的候选策略更新进行求导，学习更新系数。对 TD3+BC，分别设置自举演员和执行演员：前者抑制策略变化对目标价值的影响，后者优化局部策略改进代理目标。对 IQL，保留原有价值更新，仅调整优势加权策略提取中的逆温度参数。

Result: 在 D4RL 的 locomotion 和 AntMaze 任务上，RAPO 均提升了两种基线算法的表现；其中，针对 TD3+BC 的版本平均表现优于基线，增益也更大。

Conclusion: 策略更新应考虑动作在价值自举和执行中的不同角色。RAPO 将这两类作用分开适配，为改进离线强化学习策略正则化提供了通用思路。

Abstract: Policy regularization in offline reinforcement learning balances policy improvement against reliance on uncertain value estimates. This balance can differ between selecting actions for execution and supplying actions for critic bootstrapping, yet methods such as TD3+BC couple these roles through a shared policy. We propose Role-Adaptive Policy Optimization (RAPO), which adapts policy-update coefficients according to their roles in value learning and execution. RAPO learns these coefficients by differentiating through candidate policy updates formed using the base algorithm's actor objective. For TD3+BC, RAPO separates bootstrap and execution actors and adapts their coefficients independently: the bootstrap objective penalizes policy-induced changes in target values, while the execution objective evaluates a local policy-improvement surrogate. For IQL, whose value learning is already independent of the execution actor, RAPO preserves the original value updates and adapts only the inverse temperature in advantage-weighted policy extraction. Experiments on D4RL locomotion and AntMaze tasks show improvements over both base algorithms, with larger gains for TD3+BC, whose RAPO instantiation outperforms baselines on average.

</details>


### [139] [Game-Guided Skill Discovery through Self-Play for Playable Agent Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.40137)
*Seungeun Rho,Jeonghwan Kim,Xue Bin Peng,Sehoon Ha*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: GGSD通过自我对弈学习少量可由人直接操控的离散技能，并能将技能组合用于解决未见任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有无监督技能发现方法往往难以同时实现技能语义区分、可解释和表达能力强。研究旨在学习紧凑且适合人类直接使用的具身控制技能。

Method: 构建分层自我对弈框架：高层策略从离散技能集合中选择技能，条件化低层策略学习相应行为；训练后，人类可接管高层策略，通过同一技能集合控制智能体。技能切换还能形成超越单个动作原语的组合行为。

Result: 在 Ant、Franka 机械臂和 Unitree G1 环境中，GGSD学得的技能可由人类操控并组合使用。人类无需额外训练，即可用这些技能解决 Maze 和 CubePush 等未见任务。

Conclusion: 以竞争性游戏为基础的技能发现能够兼顾技能的可玩性、组合性与任务迁移能力，为通过少量高层行为控制具身智能体提供了可行方案。

Abstract: We present Game-Guided Skill Discovery (GGSD), a framework that uses self-play in games to discover motor skills that are directly playable by humans. Playable skills provide a compact abstraction for controlling embodied agents through a small set of learned behaviors rather than low-level actions. To be effective, these skills should be semantically distinct, interpretable, and expressive; properties that existing unsupervised skill-discovery methods often fail to achieve simultaneously. GGSD achieves these desiderata by grounding skill discovery in competitive gameplay. A hierarchical agent competes against its past selves, with a high-level policy selecting from a small discrete skill set and a skill-conditioned low-level policy learning the corresponding behaviors. After training, a human can replace the high-level policy and directly control the agent through the same discrete skills. Despite the small number of high-level actions, skill transitions give rise to emergent combo behaviors, expanding expressivity beyond individual primitives. Across Ant, Franka-arm, and Unitree G1 environments, we show that GGSD produces human-playable skills that humans can compose to solve unseen tasks, such as Maze and CubePush, without additional training. An interactive demo is available at this https URL .

</details>


### [140] [Learning to Explain While Planning: Rule-Aligned Diffusion Planning for Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39995)
*Jiaxi Ye*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出规则对齐扩散规划器 RADP，将可微驾驶规则融入扩散训练，并通过 RPA 估计各规则对轨迹优化的影响；nuPlan 实验显示其改善了复杂场景规划，规则归因与后续风险具有时间一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有扩散规划器主要从专家示范中学习统计关联，缺少对驾驶规则的显式建模。长尾场景中示范不足可能导致不符合规则的轨迹；同时，缺少规则层面的解释，妨碍故障诊断、安全验证和定向改进。

Method: RADP 将可微驾驶规则损失纳入扩散训练目标，使规则知识成为超越有限示范的内在行为约束。RPA 利用规则损失对预测轨迹的梯度构造监督信号，并通过轻量归因头在线估计各规则施加的优化压力。此外，提出时间风险对齐协议，检验当前规则压力能否反映后续闭环执行中的对应风险。

Result: 在 nuPlan 上，RADP 在具有挑战性的安全关键场景中提升了闭环规划表现。RPA 的规则压力归因与后续相应规则风险呈现稳定的时间对齐。

Conclusion: 将驾驶规则融入扩散规划训练有助于改善复杂场景中的规划行为；基于规则压力的归因则提供了与后续风险相关的规则级解释，为规划器评估和改进提供支持。

Abstract: Diffusion planners exhibit strong capabilities in generating multimodal trajectories. However, existing methods primarily rely on expert demonstrations to fit trajectory distributions, learning statistical correlations among scenes, behaviors, and trajectories without explicitly modeling driving rules. In long-tail scenarios where expert data are scarce, the lack of behaviors to imitate may lead to trajectories that violate safety or compliance requirements. Moreover, their generation process lacks rule-level explanations, making it difficult to determine which rules drive trajectory adjustments, when they take effect, and how strongly they act, thereby limiting failure diagnosis, safety validation, and targeted improvement. To address these limitations, we propose the Rule-Aligned Diffusion Planner (RADP), which incorporates differentiable driving rules into the diffusion objective during training, turning rule knowledge into intrinsic behavioral principles beyond finite demonstrations. We further introduce Rule-Pressure Attribution (RPA), which constructs supervision signals from gradients of rule losses with respect to predicted trajectories and employs a lightweight attribution head to estimate the optimization pressure exerted by each rule online. To assess the closed-loop behavioral relevance of these attributions, we propose a temporal risk-alignment protocol that evaluates whether current rule pressures reflect corresponding risks during subsequent closed-loop execution. Experiments on nuPlan show that RADP improves closed-loop planning in challenging safety-critical scenarios, while RPA exhibits consistent temporal alignment with subsequent rule-specific risks, validating both intrinsic rule learning and rule-level interpretability.

</details>


### [141] [Do Better Goal Representations Improve Goal-Conditioned Reinforcement Learning?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39901)
*Syed Nazmus Sakib,Abdul Monaf Chowdhury,Nafiul Haque,Shifat E Arman,Md Mehedi Hasan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 在离线目标条件强化学习的导航任务中，当前状态的表示质量比目标表示质量更能影响性能；随机傅里叶位置编码可提升困难任务表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 目标表示常被认为是目标条件强化学习性能的关键，但表示质量对下游表现的实际影响尚不明确。研究旨在比较目标表示与当前状态表示的作用。

Method: 在确定性迷宫中构造精确的时间距离目标表示，并逐步破坏其几何质量，同时固定下游学习器；在 OGBench 导航任务和两种算法上评估。另对当前状态表示施加相同干预，并测试随机傅里叶位置编码。

Result: 显著改变目标表示质量几乎不影响性能；对当前状态表示进行干预则使成功率提升超过一倍。随机傅里叶位置编码无需地图信息或修改优化目标，即可改善最困难导航任务的表现。

Conclusion: 在基于状态的离线导航中，优化智能体当前状态的表示通常比精细化目标表示更重要；随机傅里叶位置编码是一种简单有效的改进方案。

Abstract: Goal-conditioned reinforcement learning (GCRL) relies heavily on how target goals are represented to the policy. While recent methods encode goals via temporal distance, occupancy, or controllability, it remains unclear how much downstream performance actually depends on representation quality. We study this in offline GCRL by constructing an exact temporal-distance goal representation in deterministic mazes. We then systematically corrupt its geometric quality while keeping the downstream learner fixed. Across OGBench navigation tasks and two algorithms, large changes in goal-representation quality produce almost no change in performance. However, applying the same interventions to the agent's current state more than doubles success, revealing the state pathway as the true bottleneck. Building on this insight, we show that simple random Fourier positional encodings substantially improve performance on the hardest navigation tasks without map information or objective modifications. Overall, our findings suggest that in state-based offline navigation, improving how the agent's current state is represented matters far more than refining the goal representation. Code will be released soon.

</details>


### [142] [ReSAIL: Mitigating Collapse in Iterative Agent Self-Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39306)
*Shengjie Jin,Hengbo Xu,Zelong Sun,YuJie Guo,Zhiwu Lu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: ReSAIL通过选择高信息量交互步骤并保留特权信息（PI）条件行为，缓解迭代自蒸馏中智能体部署性能逐轮下降的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有迭代自蒸馏方法在连续部署周期中出现性能坍塌，使用特权信息时的任务表现也会下降，削弱了递归自我改进的效果。

Method: 提出即插即用增强方法ReSAIL，用于基于PI的迭代自蒸馏。该方法优先选择PI对教师预测影响最大的交互步骤，平衡不同轨迹的蒸馏损失；同时将学生在所选及未选步骤上的PI条件输出分布正则化，使其接近冻结教师，以保留后续周期监督所需的行为。

Result: 在ALFWorld和TextCraft上，ReSAIL在不同模型规模下连续三个周期保持显著收益；相较自蒸馏基线，最终周期成功率的平均绝对提升为22.5%。在AITZ上，基于敏感度筛选离线数据也提升了多模态GUI智能体的动作预测准确率。

Conclusion: 研究表明，通过有针对性地选择蒸馏交互并保留PI条件行为，可以有效缓解部署轨迹上的迭代自蒸馏性能坍塌，为更稳健的智能体迭代学习提供了实验证据。

Abstract: Iterative self-distillation enables LLM agents to learn from successive deployments, offering a path toward recursive self-improvement (RSI). Yet our experiments with existing methods reveal a collapse in deployment performance across cycles, while task performance with privileged information (PI) also declines. We address this collapse by prioritizing informative interaction steps for distillation and preserving PI-conditioned behavior as the student becomes the next teacher. We introduce Retentive and Selective Augmentation for Iterative Self-Distillation (ReSAIL), a plug-in augmentation for iterative PI-based self-distillation. ReSAIL selects interaction steps where PI most strongly changes the teacher's predictions and balances the resulting distillation losses across trajectories. It also regularizes the student's PI-conditioned output distributions toward those of the frozen teacher at selected and unselected steps to preserve PI-conditioned behavior for supervision in the next cycle. On ALFWorld and TextCraft, ReSAIL sustains substantial gains across model scales over three cycles, with an average absolute gain of 22.5% in final-cycle success rates when added to self-distillation baselines. Sensitivity-guided selection of offline data also improves action prediction accuracy for multimodal GUI agents on AITZ. These findings provide the first evidence that a more robust learning mechanism can effectively mitigate performance collapse in iterative agent self-distillation over deployment trajectories.

</details>


### [143] [Shared Phase and Retention Control for Efficient Adaptive Spectral Recurrence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39082)
*Wentao Wang,Hengyu Zhong,Yunhan Jiang,Jialiang An,Meng Lu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SPARC以两个输入相关的共享标量信号调节谱递归记忆，在保持固定状态规模的同时实现自适应记忆控制，并提升多项任务表现与运行效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Transformer的计算与缓存开销随上下文长度增长；固定状态递归模型虽可实现常量内存推理，却通常采用静态状态转移，难以动态调整记忆衰减或相位旋转。现有选择性架构则为每个记忆模式配置独立控制，控制成本随状态容量增长。

Method: 提出共享相位与保留控制方法SPARC，使用两个输入相关的标量信号，协调不同复数记忆模式的保留和相位旋转，同时保留各模式特有的基线时间尺度与频率。其对角仿射递归支持并行关联扫描和精确的结构化实时递归学习（RTRL）。

Result: 在部分可观测连续控制、POPGym和序列分类任务中，SPARC相较次优方法在Walker-P上提升9.09%的相对回报，在FordA上提升1.36%的相对准确率。NVIDIA Blackwell GPU上的实现使固定token工作负载的递归混合器训练延迟降低18.2%–34.2%，扫描速度达到优化RG-LRU基线的3.1–4.7倍。

Conclusion: 实验表明，仅用两个共享控制信号即可有效调节高维谱记忆，并兼顾在线学习与全序列训练，为高效自适应递归模型提供了一种控制开销较低的方案。

Abstract: As new evidence arrives, a sequence model must update what it remembers and how memory influences predictions. While Transformers incur computation and cache costs scaling with context length, fixed-state recurrent models offer constant-memory inference.
